Left `INCLUDE_ASM` (not T-0018 unless a row exists in [[data/t0018-cases]]; 13 rows added):
- GEKO func_8013FF64: all but the position of `move s0,zero` (the permuter's score-0 candidate puts `i = 0; do { ... i += 1;` of the second loop on ONE line: as1 line records again, T-3330 "line breaks change as1's schedule"); not taken.
- RPG_BAT func_8013C1CC: original leaves `lw; nop` and `bnez; nop` slots unfilled in the first three tests of masked globals; IDO fills them (not a register gap).
- EVENT func_80100DD8: lw of D_800EECBC hoisted above lhu.
- TACO func_80141050/80141170/80141290 (slot allocator with a `p`, `i`, actor pointer live across `func_801335A0`): the original spills the three locals at sp+0x2C/0x34/0x38 around the call and reloads them after; the plain C keeps a2/p in other slots and spills arg1/arg0 too (frame 0x40 vs 0x38). Not matched.
- main `load_tpage_buf_lock`: a K&R definition (`arg2` as `u8 arg2;`) reproduces the original frame (all three arguments saved at sp+0x38.., `lbu a2,0x40(sp)`), since main_api.h has the function unprototyped; the remaining diff is the original's `move t9,a0 / move a1,t9 / andi t0,a1,0x1F / move a1,t0` shuffle of `arg0 & 0x1F` and temp numbering. Not matched.
- main func_80048EB8: save/restore of three s16 globals through stack temporaries around a loop; the original frame is 0x48, the plain C 0x40 (two extra words). Not matched.
- OPTION func_80139E3C: pointer table via D_8013D360, u16 counter in $a3, D_800E71DF (u8) kept in $t2 for the third argument; permuter base score 1775, plain C differs in every register. Not matched.
- DATE func_8015A640 (rotation of a 3x2 s16 matrix with the sine table at D_800DE500): the original is not IDO output (`addu t7,a0,zero` moves, `multu` followed by two nops, no scheduling across statements); it looks like an SDK-style object compiled with gcc + maspsx. Left INCLUDE_ASM.
- ENDING func_80139B48 (7 arguments, packet of 12 byte/halfword stores): after fixing the p slot (pad above p, FAKE) and CSE of the D_800E6280 address (`(u8 *)&D_800E6280`), the original still keeps the colour argument in $a0/$t6/$t7 and p in $a1, IDO uses $v0/$v1/$a0; permuter scores >1600. Not matched.

Not matched.
- RPG_BAT func_801495DC: the original spills two locals (sp2C/sp30, frame 0x38) and computes `(x * 9) / 2` as `addiu at,t6,1; sra` from a value it multiplies elsewhere; plain C frame is 0x30 with another multiply sequence. Not matched.
- DATE func_80149F48 (history shift of three words: `D_8015D078 = D_8015D06C`, ..., `D_8015D084 = idx * 3 + D`): the original loads each source one statement ahead of its store and keeps the s16 `D_8015D084` in $v0 (later reused for the u8 index), IDO hoists differently; same shape as the load-hoisting cases of EVENT/ETC but the one-base-symbol trick does not help (words at +0x00..+0x14 of `D_8015D06C`). Not matched.
- EVENT func_80102118 (0x9C table, debug printf, then a range check on the selector): bare table sits at sp+0x2C, the original has it at sp+0x28 with the same frame; any extra local (s32, u8, s16, before or after) grows the frame by 8. No FAKE pad reproduces it. Not matched.
## Wave 3, list 8 (T-4080): 30 functions in 800451D0, 8007C030 and 17 overlay files
Ticket [[tickets/T-4080-wave-3-list-8]]. New patterns:
- Overlay function names are not unique, and `funcdiff.py func_X` resolves the name to the first C file that holds it, not to the overlay being edited. It printed `MATCH` for two functions whose overlay failed sha1 (TAIIKU `func_801388E4`, GYOZI `func_8013D9E8`). The sha1 line of `ninja` decides; to see a diff, compare `build/ovl/<OVL>/src/ovl/<OVL>/<addr>.o` against `expected/build/ovl/...` with `objdump -dr`.
- Table copy with an index: the idiom of T-3330 matched 7 more (`GYOZI func_80139D34, func_8013C670, func_8013D5B0, func_801388E0, func_80139920`, `GEKO func_8013D74C, func_801338F0`, `BUNKAKEN func_80145E40`, `SHOUGATU func_8013A1CC`). A table whose frame has two words above it (TAIIKU `func_80133C80`) needs a second unused local declared before the table; it is marked `FAKE`. The same happens for a dead struct copy (EVENT `func_800FE090`: 0x80-byte copy to a local, frame 4 bytes larger than IDO gives).
- Debug `printf` between the table copy and the call (EVENT `func_800FF770`, `func_800FF82C`): `tbl = D_x; func_80078950("..."); idx = D_800B1AF6; tbl.f[idx](0x80);` matches; the index is read after the call.
- Post-increment of a `lw` global in a compare (`don_wait`: `(u32)D_800E7384++ >= 0x81U`) matches; `D += 1; if (D ...)` does not (the original compares the old value in `$v1`). `if (D++ == 1 && ...)` does not match (`func_80135A6C`: the original builds the flag with `xori; sltiu 1; beqz`).
- An uninitialised local that is only written in some branches (TAIIKU `func_801388E4`: `slot`) is kept in memory; to put it at `sp+0x10` declare the CSE temporary (`key = arg0 + 1`) first, then `slot` (first declared = highest address).
- A temporary reload after the assignment (`cur = D_80144C50; if (cur == 0x64)`) changes the load order of two globals (GEKO `func_80133F94`, found by the permuter, plain C).
- `TcActor` switch functions (TACO `func_80157CD4`, `func_80157EA8`): the `addu` operand order follows the common-subexpression group. Write `(arg0 + D_8015EDB4)->f` for the switch value, the `+=` reads and the if/`+= 1` pair, and `D_8015EDB4[arg0].f = ...` for the plain stores (`unk78`, `unk7A = 0`). Mixing them per statement was needed; all-one-form misses by one `addu`.
- Unsigned compare of a `u8` against a constant needs a `U` suffix on the constant (`D_800E738A < 0xA0U` gives `sltiu`), shifting a `u8` to extract a nibble needs a `(u32)` cast (`srl`, not `sra`), and the order of two global loads in one expression follows the source order of the operands (GYOZI `func_8013DA58`: swap the operands to match).
- A `u8` parameter used as a divisor adds `move v0,a1` and renumbers the temporaries (TACO `func_801368D8`), and `x = f() & 0x7F` followed by a compare chain keeps the copy `move v1,v0; andi t6,v1,0x7F` in the original (GEKO `func_80136F20`, `func_80137040`, `func_801331A0`, main `func_80083628`). Rows are in [[data/t0018-cases]].
- Left as `INCLUDE_ASM`, not T-0018: struct arrays indexed by `* 0x38` where the original uses `multu` with the element size in `$a0` (GEKO `func_801374A8`, SHOUGATU `func_80142784`; a `Rec38` typedef did not change it); `D_800E6448` flag tests where the original uses `sll 28; bgez` (a bit-field read, GEKO `func_80136E7C`); a call whose arguments the original schedules differently (main `func_800463E8`, 7 arguments); two `s16[2]` arrays passed to `menu_set` (`normal_date_two_select_init`: frame 8 bytes larger in the original, constants kept in `$v0/$v1`); a counted loop of 13 stores that IDO unrolls with the stores in another order (NAME_ENT `func_80142A3C`); an `sprite_brightness(i, ...)` call in a loop where the callee's visible prototype adds a narrowing of `i` (main `func_80085368`, `func_800853FC`); the 4-flag function NAME_ENT `func_8013B0A0` (the `-0x81` mask constant is materialised in another block).
## Wave 3, list 9 (T-4090): 27 files, main exe and 16 overlays
Ticket [[tickets/T-4090-wave3-list-9]]. 59 of the 136 functions (8984 of 26284 bytes) matched; clean build 27 of 27 sha1 OK, `ninja progress` grand total 2805 -> 2864 of 6958 functions. Matched from the list: table-copy functions (EVENT, GYOZI, DATE, SHOUGATU, GEKO, MASTER, DATE2, OMIMAI, TAIIKU), string setters, the DATE screen set-up family, three jump-table switches, ETC bit-field loop, EN_NICHI record loops. Failed attempts that fit the T-0018 shapes are rows in [[data/t0018-cases]]. New or confirmed patterns:
- **Table copy, which scalar first.** A function-pointer table whose size is a multiple of 8 (GEKO `func_8013BBE0` 0xE8, MASTER `func_80138E58` a 12-byte pointer table, DATE2 `func_80136FC4`) needs one 4-byte scalar declared before it, as in T-3330; if the index global is loaded twice in the original (once for a compare, once for the call, GEKO `func_8013BBE0`, DATE `func_8014FBA0` stays open) the scalar is an unused local, if the index is loaded once it is `idx = D_xxx; tbl.f[idx]()`. A real counter that is declared first does the same job (TAIIKU `func_80144834`, which has a local `s32 tbl[3]` copy and a three-record loop). Tables whose size is 4 mod 8 and whose index is read twice (DATE `func_8014FBA0`, 0xAC bytes) land at sp+0x2C or sp+0x30; the original has sp+0x28: not reproduced.
- **Implicit int is the first thing to try on a near miss.** `s32 f(void)` without a `return` value (or with a bare `return;`) stops as1 from filling a branch delay slot with the next block's `lui`/`addiu` and changes how uopt compiles a `D++ >= N` / `D++ == N` test at the end of a conditional tail: DATE `func_8014FDA0` (`lw ra` stays in the slot), EN_NICHI `func_80132B40` (permuter, score 60 to 0), DATE `func_8014FD30` and ETC `func_8014A180` (the original's `sltiu`/`xori` + `beqz` instead of IDO's `bnez`; the row for `func_8014FD30` in [[data/t0018-cases]] was written before this and stays). The permuter finds it from a `void` base because it tries return types; try it by hand before the permuter. GEKO `func_8013BEE0` needed it too (with the dead second `D != 0` test of `D == 0 || (D != 0 && f() == 9)`). It did not help EN_NICHI `func_80134784`, the `u8`/`s16` clamp and sign-extension cases (EVENT `func_8010A790`, GYOZI `func_801427E4`) or DATE `func_8014F370`.
- **Load kept after a store (FAKE, same family as ENDING `func_80136C5C`).** Address the *later* statement through the earlier symbol: `(&D_800E643E)[4] -= 0x14;` for the second and third read-modify-write of adjacent halfwords (DATE `func_8014F404`, `func_8014F500`, `func_8014F610`, `func_8014F7F4`), `(&D_800E62B6)[1] += 2;` (EN_NICHI `func_80132B40`, `func_80132C4C`). When the earlier statement is a constant store and the later one a load of another symbol (SHUGAKU `func_801351CC`), store *through the symbol of the later load* with a negative index, `(&D_800E71DF)[-0x83D] = 0;`; indexing from the stored symbol does nothing.
- **Strings.** In an island object (`config/objects/*.txt` `yes`) the literal is written inline and `funcdiff.py` only shows the `.rodata` relocation pair; `build/ovl/<NAME>.bin` sha1 is the check. `printf`-style callees need a K&R declaration (`func_800AE0B0()` was `(void *)`; the call passes `D_800E6377` as second argument).
- **Jump tables in a plain function.** ETC `func_8013BD40` (cases 1-5, 10-12 and 6-9 as two groups), main `func_8007A618` (explicit empty `case 0:` to `case 5:` labels give the original's table of 0..15 and its `sltiu 16`), `func_8007B99C` all match as soon as the labels are written out; the funcdiff text only differs by the table relocation.
- **Renamed callees.** m2c prints the old `func_XXXXXXXX` names; the symbol file renames them (`config/obin_renames.txt`: `func_80043914` is `load_palette`, `func_80084D3C` is `check_para_limit`, `func_8004E788` is `set_kanji_string`, `func_8004E884` is `k_disp_start`, `func_8007EAAC` is `normal_date_move_place`). Use the new name or the link fails; do not declare the old name in `main_api.h`.
- **Result of a `void` callee passed on.** ENDING `func_8013BDD8` passes the return value of `set_kanji_string` to `k_disp_start`; matched with `#define MAIN_API_OVERRIDE_set_kanji_string` in `ENDING.h` and `s32 set_kanji_string(s16, s32, s32, void *, s32)` (the `s16` gives the `sll/sra` of the first argument).
- **Prototype width decides the call code.** `func_80044750` returns 1 and calls `func_80079B10(u16)` with the prototype (`andi t6,a0,0xffff; move a0,t6`); the same body with a K&R callee gives `andi a0,a0,0xffff`. `main_api.h` now has `s32 func_80044750(s32)` and `void func_80079B10(u16)`.
- **Bit-field tests.** ETC `func_80149468` (four-word flag array, eleven entries): a `typedef struct { u32 pad0:25; u32 grade:2; u32 pad1:3; u32 flag:1; u32 pad2:1; }` read as `((EtcSlot *)(D_800E6280 + 0x66C + i * 4))->grade >= 3 && ->flag` gives `sll 5; srl 30` and `sll 1; bgez`; the base must be `D_800E6280 + 0x66C + i * 4` (the loop pointer then starts at D_800E6280+0xC as in the original, three peeled iterations first).
- **Record loops over `D_8011ECD0` (0x44-byte records).** EN_NICHI `func_80132C4C`/`func_80132B40`: `for (i = 0; i < 64; i++) D_8011ECD0[0x1987 + i * 0x44] -= 4;` is unrolled by IDO exactly like the original (four stores after one `addiu 0x110`); the field offsets in m2c's draft are relative to the already advanced pointer, add 0x110.
- **Local struct copies are not always T-3330.** `Tri3 tbl` (12 bytes) copies in a three-record loop: the frame of the original has no stack slot for the other scalars, so the loop variable has to be the only other local (extra pointer or temp locals move the table up by 4 per local). TAIIKU `func_80144B40` (three constants in $s0/$s1/$t regs) stays open for that reason, `func_801448D4` matches except for temp numbers.

- **decomp-permuter paid off on small near misses** (`permute.py <func> --time 200`, three at a time in one container each, about 4 of 15 reached score 0): ETC `func_8014A180`, DATE `func_8014FD30` (return type, above), GYOZI `func_80135CA0` (an empty `if (!D && !D) {}` after the table-slot store; FAKE), GYOZI `func_80135D64` (`D == (D_800F563A * 0)`; FAKE), GYOZI `func_801361D4` (`u32 v = (D_800F62CF = D_800F5ACD); f(v);` is plain), GYOZI `func_801427E4` (the global is `volatile s16`: declared so in `GYOZI.h`), main `func_8007BE94` (`(arg0 ^ 0) == (u16) D`; FAKE: the left operand becomes an expression and uopt swaps the compare), main `func_80057640` (`dec_bg_show_set(1, (u32) (1 - D_800B5939))` recomputes the value for the argument instead of passing the local; FAKE). A base score of 0 in the permuter dir means the C already matches; scores that stay above 200 after 300 s were never worth more time (EN_NICHI `func_80135A48`, `func_80133924`, TT `func_8013ADC0`, main `func_80079C70`, `func_8007A50C`, `func_8007B64C`, `load_palette` best 60).

Left as `INCLUDE_ASM`, not T-0018 (the others are rows in [[data/t0018-cases]]):
- Shared constants: `D = 4; f(4)` style (EVENT `func_8010B614`), one `li` for several stores (GYOZI `func_8014210C`, four equal `sh` groups), a mask constant shared by `&= 0x7FFFFFFF` and `|= 0x80000000` (EVENT `func_8010B570`, `func_8010B6E4`, `func_8010B8A4`; T-3001).
- Four read-modify-write halfwords in a row (EVENT `func_8010B678`): the loads of the third and fourth are hoisted in the original; the index trick does not reproduce that pattern.
- TACO `func_8015D270` is SDK-style code (`multu` for signed products, `nop` after `mflo`): gcc, not IDO.
- Frames that need a spill of a computed pointer across a call without an `s` register (EN_NICHI `func_80134F2C`, TT `func_8013A610`/`func_8013A710` with locals at sp+0x30/0x5C).
- A base address that stays in a register for `D_8014A13C.field` accesses (TAIIKU `func_80143D24`) cannot be forced with a cast or a local pointer; constant offsets fold into `%lo`.

Tooling: `tools/permute.py all <func>` does not accept the documented positional form (`permute.py: error: unrecognized arguments`); `permute.py <func> --time N` works. `tools/sync_protos.py --fix` took more than ten minutes with other agents building; adding the declarations by hand to `main_api.h` (sorted by address) and running `tools/check_headers.py include src` is equivalent and instant. `git checkout include/main_api.h` throws away every other agent-visible addition of the session: never use it to undo one declaration.
## Wave 3, list 5 (T-4050): 67 functions in 27 files
Ticket [[tickets/T-4050-wave-3-list-5]]. 63 of the 134 listed functions (8676 of 26044 bytes) and 4 more from `queue.py --by bytes` (EVENT, DATE2, VALEN) matched: main `80041000`, DATE, DATE2, EVENT, GYOZI, MASTER, TACO, TEL, TT, ETC, BUNKA_SD, VALEN. 37 rows added to [[data/t0018-cases]]. Method per function: `m2c.py --unit`, edit, `ninja <object>`, `funcdiff.py --resolve --no-build --built <object>`, then `objdump -dr` of both objects when the diff is only an order (funcdiff lists a moved instruction as one `-` and one `+` line far apart).

What worked (in the order of how often it paid off):
- **Local function-pointer tables (T-3330) again**, 26 functions. Beyond the idiom: the table size decides where the table sits. A bare table of a size that is 4 mod 8 sits at sp+0x2C, one with the index local declared first at sp+0x28 (EVENT `func_800FD4FC` 0xCC, `func_800FD6E0` 0xA4, `func_8011AB00` copy of 0x2A bytes = 21 `u16`). The copy size is the loop (3 words per pass) plus the tail: a tail of `lw/sw` at +0 and +4 means 8 more bytes (`func_800FD6E0`, m2c prints the loop size only). A table call that has other statements around it reads the index global directly, no `idx` local, when the original reloads it (`func_800FD4FC`: `idx` is still needed for the frame, assign it just before the call).
- **Bit-field tests.** `sll rX,rY,N; bgez/bltz` on a word is a bit-field read, also for flags that C would write as `x & 0x4000`: `typedef struct { u32 pad : 14; u32 b14 : 1; u32 rest : 17; }` and `((Flags14 *)&D_800E6560)->b14` (DATE `func_80156570`, `func_801565B4` with `!`). Several flags of one word share a struct (TEL `CharFlagsTel`: b1, b12, b13; `TelEntryFlags`: a 4-bit field at bit 25 and bit 29, `(u32)(x << 3) >> 28` is that nibble; EVENT `EvFlags` bits 12, 13). Pointer to the word from an `u8` array: `(EvFlags *)(D_800B0A10 + D_800B1746 * 0x34)`. `TEL.h` does not include `game.h`, so the struct is local to the C file.
- **`D++ == 0` compares the old value.** `sltiu v0,v1,1` followed by `addiu v1,v1,1; sw` is `if (D_800B1AF0++ == 0)`, not `D += 1; if (D == 0)` (EVENT `func_80118418`, `func_801184DC`, `func_8011A1E4`; m2c prints the second form).
- **`(u8)f(x) & 0x7F` or a `u8` local.** `v = (u8)f() & 0x7F` reserves the extra temporary the original has (DATE2 `func_801366E8`); a `u8 v = f() & 0x7F` local with `v >= 2U` gives `sltiu` and puts the `andi` into the right temporary (EVENT `func_8011760C`, `func_80116620`). A plain `u32`/`s32` local puts it into a saved register or `$v1`. `sltiu` needs an unsigned constant (`2U`) when the value is a `u8`.
- **A local copy of a global hoists its `lui`.** `u32 v = D_800B1746;` assigned right after an earlier store gives the original's `lui v0` position (EVENT `func_80116888`; `u8`/`s32` do not).
- **Implicit int return.** A `bnez`/`beqz` with a `nop` in its delay slot where IDO would put the next `lui` there means `v0` is live at the end: declare the function `s32` and give it no `return` (BUNKA_SD `func_801354C8`; DATE2 `func_801366E8` returns `0` on the early path only).
- **Unused parameters as locals, leaf functions.** The original keeps loop variables in `$a0/$a1` when the function has no parameters at all; `void f(s32 i, u8 *p)` reproduces that (TT `func_8013F988`, FAKE). It does not work in non-leaf functions (the parameters are spilled to their home slots).
- **A call with an argument the function never sets** (`a0` untouched, `a1 = 1`) forwards the incoming register: `s32 f(s32 arg0)` with `g(arg0, 1)` (DATE2 `func_801365DC`).
- **Variadic callee.** m2c drops the arguments of `printf`-style calls; look for `move a1,sN` before the `jal`. `func_800AE0B0` is now `void func_800AE0B0(void *arg0, ...)` in `main_api.h`; its other callers still match. `for (i = 0, p = base; i != 8; i++, p += 4)` instead of `do {} while` changed the preheader order of the hoisted string address and matched TEL `func_80139920`.
- **Strings.** String literals in objects with an island (`config/objects/*.txt`) match when written as literals in UTF-8; `funcdiff.py` reports the relocation as `.rodata+off` against `D_xxxxxxxx` and calls it a DIFF. Only the overlay sha1 decides (EVENT, VALEN, DATE2, TEL checked).
- **Folded constants.** `(arg0 + 0x60) * 0x44` is folded to `arg0 * 0x44 + 0x1980` and CSE'd with the first multiply; a local `s32 j = arg0 + 0x60;` keeps three real multiplies (ETC `func_8014387C`). Matching unsigned compare chains need `0x2U`, `v >= 1` instead of `v != 0` for `sltiu/xori`.

Fakematches used (all marked `FAKE`, same family as the earlier "index the first symbol" tricks):
- A load that as1 hoists above an earlier store: read or write the neighbouring global through the first symbol, `(&D_800E65C6)[2] += 2;` (DATE `func_80153928`, `func_8015347C`, `func_80153E90` also stores `D_800E681D` as `*((s8 *)&D_800E65CA + 0x253)`), `(&D_800B0A06)[2]` (EVENT `func_80119A60`), `(&D_800B1746)[0x3B0] += 0xF` for `D_800B1AF6` (EVENT `func_8011B09C`), `(&D_800E699E)[0x9EC]` for `D_800E738A` (DATE2 `func_801366E8`).
- IDO keeps a `u8` global in a register across byte or halfword stores through the same array and the original reloads it: build the store address through `(u32)`: `*(u8 *)((u32)D_800E6280 + D_800E71DE * 8 + 0x75E) = x;` (MASTER `func_80138374`) and `(u8 *)((u32)D_800B0860 + D_800B1746 * 0x34)` (EVENT `func_8011B09C`). A pointer variable that is assigned the array address is not enough; IDO knows what it points to.
- `s32 pad;` before a `RECT` (DATE2 `func_80132F48`, same frame gap as the T-2040 note).

Left as `INCLUDE_ASM`, new causes (the rows are in [[data/t0018-cases]]):
- **Constant per compare, EVENT.** A chain of `==` compares against the same constant (`1`, `2`, `3`) has `li at,N` before each in the original; IDO keeps the constant in `$v0`/`$v1` and swaps the `beq` operands (`func_800FD360`, `func_8011A508`, `func_80117A68`, `func_80119824`, `func_80119440`). Ten halfword stores of 2 and 3 share two registers in the original, IDO loads per store (`func_80118868`).
- **Delay slot choice.** The original fills a `jal` delay slot with the stack-argument store (`sw zero,0x10(sp)`, TACO `func_80152110`); a final `jr` delay slot with `or v0,a0` while the branches keep `nop` (OPTION `func_8013A550`); a post-increment compare is materialised with `xori; sltiu; beqz` where IDO branches on the `xori` result (GYOZI `func_8013AA48`).
- **Loops that IDO unrolls and the original does not** (main `func_800415B4`, ETC `func_80143240`): plain index loop, pointer loop and `do {} while` are all unrolled by 4 with a remainder.
- **Promoted globals, both ways.** `reverse` rows: the original reloads where IDO keeps one register (DATE `func_801563A8`, `func_8015908C`, `func_801591BC`; EVENT `func_80119370`, `func_800FA6A0`; GYOZI `func_8013E638`; TT `func_8013F878`, where the u16 field is reloaded at the end of a then-block and after a `-= 1`; main `func_80041F48`, base address of `D_800E6280` hoisted over five loops). `promo` rows: GYOZI `func_80140780` (return value in `$v0` vs `$v1`), EVENT `func_800FD588`.
- **Temporaries one register low, frames 8 bytes bigger:** GYOZI `func_8013BC40`/`func_8013BCB4`, TEL `func_80139E3C`, `func_8013BE38`, EVENT `func_800FA0D0`/`func_800FA194` (a0-a3 used as loop registers in a non-leaf function), TACO `func_80137CBC`.
- A redundant second store the original keeps (GYOZI `func_80135988`), a block copy whose tail `lw` IDO hoists (EVENT `func_800FAE6C`), an `s16` local in `$a0` (DATE2 `func_801338F8`), three small diffs in DATE2 `func_80136794`, VALEN `func_80133670` (call argument loaded through a `lui` that sits in the previous delay slot).

Tooling notes (no tool changed): (1) `funcdiff.py <name>` without `--built` takes the first C file that mentions the name, which is the wrong overlay for shared addresses; I called it as `funcdiff.py --resolve --no-build --built <object> <name>` after `ninja <object>` (a wrapper that stops when ninja fails, since `--no-build` skips funcdiff's own build check). (2) `funcdiff.py --resolve` prints a moved instruction as unrelated `-`/`+` lines, so a pure scheduling difference looks like a handful of register lines; compare `objdump -dr` of the two objects (`expected/build/...` and `build/...`) when the listed lines look like the same instructions. (3) `permute.py` finds the function by name over all C files; for names that exist in several overlays as `INCLUDE_ASM` it can pick another overlay's C (TEL `func_80139E3C` scored 4705), and for functions with strings the relocation `.rodata` against `D_xxxxxxxx` makes every score non-zero. (4) `sync_protos.py --write` re-sorts a few existing lines in `main_api.h` (renamed symbols); harmless.
## Wave 3, list 3 (T-4030): 41 functions in 20 files (38 from the list, 5464 bytes, plus GYOZI `func_801435C4`/`801439F0` and ENDING `func_80133738`, 380 bytes, from the byte queue)
Ticket [[tickets/T-4030-wave-3-list-3]]. Matched: GYOZI `func_80143110`/`80143314`/`801433E0`/`80143520`/`80143940`, ENDING `func_8013363C`/`801336A8`, TT `func_80132130`, TAIIKU `func_8013D254`/`80138A34`/`8013B0AC`/`8013FE44`, DATE `func_80154C84`/`80154D38`/`80154E14`, SHOUGATU `func_80137D68`/`80137F70`/`80133610`/`8013E410`/`80133A88`, GEKO `func_801394EC`/`80142B20`, NAME_ENT `func_80137EE4`/`8013621C`/`80138268`/`80134EB8`/`80132000`, KANGEI `func_80135390`, RPG_BAT `func_801452C0`, EVENT `func_800F70F0`, main `RangeMouse`, `func_8004AC18`, `func_8004AD60`, `k_disp_inc`, `func_80046684`, `func_80059048`, `func_80059860`. Gaps that stayed open are rows in [[data/t0018-cases]] (tagged "w3-3").

New patterns that worked:
- **Cluster of neighbouring globals as one struct or array stops as1 hoisting a load over an earlier store.** The original keeps source order where IDO hoists `lui/lw` of the second global above the store to the first (the "load hoisted above an earlier store" family of batch I). Declaring the cluster as one object makes IDO keep the order: RPG_BAT `func_801452C0` (`RpgStat` in `include/ovl/RPG_BAT.h`, three counters and one at +0x38), DATE `func_80154D38` (`s16 D_800E6476[]`, fields at [0], [2], [4]), SHOUGATU `func_80133A88` (`s16 D_800E643E[]`). Operand order of a commutative sum can change with arrays (an `a + b + c` over three globals flipped to `b + a + c`), so check the first lines of the diff. Not enough for the `$at` sharing cases (one `lui at` over several stores): a struct, an array and `(&D)[n]` all re-emit `lui at` (`k_reset`, `k_disp_start`, TAIIKU `func_8013A63C`).
- **`D++ == 0` as post-increment** gives the original's `sltiu v0,v1,1; addiu v1; beqz` (KANGEI `func_80135390`). With a non-zero constant IDO emits `xori; bnez` instead (SHUGAKU `func_80134024` stays open).
- **`u8 kind = f() & 0x7F; if (G || kind == 4)`**: a `u8` local keeps the masked value in `$t8` where an `s32` local lands in `$t7` (SHOUGATU `func_8013E410`).
- **Second local with a real use for the frame**: `func_8004AC18` needed `level = arg0; ... color = level * 0x10101` to get the spill slot at sp+0x38 (a single local gave +0x3C). Same rule as T-3330: the first declared local takes the highest slot.
- **Two-word T-3330 case (TAIIKU `func_80138A34`, 0x24-byte table with 8 bytes above it)**: `s32 idx; s32 pad; FnTbl9 tbl;` matches; `pad` is a FAKE-marked unused local. ENDING `func_801336A8` is the RECT variant (`s32 pad; RECT rect;`, 4 bytes more above the RECT), also FAKE.
- **Unrolled counted loops** (IDO unrolls `for (i = 0; i < N; i++)` by 4 when N is constant): write the loop with the original trip count; pointer-walk loops (`p != end`) are not unrolled. BCD decode `func_80046684`, `func_80059048` (16 words at stride 4), NAME_ENT `func_80137EE4` (the pre-incremented pointer means record offsets are written +0x44 with i from 0).
- **Operand order of `multu`** depends on whether the global was read into a local first: `u32 sx = D_800E36E8; D = arg * sx;` gives `multu a0,v0` (`RangeMouse`), `arg * D` gives `multu v0,a0`.
- **Statement order and as1**: `func_80059860` needs `[0]`, `[4]`, `[8]` stores in that order so that the `li -0x100` is scheduled after the second `lui`; stores out of order in the C (field `[8]` second) hoist the `li`.
- **String literals in island objects** work as written (`func_80137F70`, `func_80133610`, `func_80154E14`); the matching rodata chunk comes from the C object.
- A second symbol as loop base: `func_8013FE44` reads `D_801499B6 + 4*i`, so the table has to be declared as `TaiikuPair D_801499B4[]` (own symbol at +4); indexing `D_801499B0[i + 1]` gives `lui/addiu` of another address and fails the sha1.

Left open (new instances of known gaps, rows in [[data/t0018-cases]]): `$v1` instead of `$v0` for a promoted global or return value (DATE `func_801551D0`, SHOUGATU `func_80137DE4`, NAME_ENT `func_80134110`, SHUGAKU `func_80134024`); the switch/compare selector in `$t7`/`$t6` (SHOUGATU `func_801345C8`, RPG_BAT `func_80144B7C`); `lui at` sharing and load hoisting (`k_reset`, TAIIKU `func_8013C5C8`, `func_8013A63C`, KANGEI `func_80134EA8`); the constant `0xFF` kept in `$v0` (KANGEI `func_80134120`); `multu` for a 0x38-byte struct index through `ixa` (KANGEI `func_80135438`); a `RECT` whose stores come out as h, w, x, y (main `func_80059308`); argument registers `$a0/$a1` rewritten after a `u16` mask (TT `func_8013C764`); GPU packet with a bitfield tag (TT `func_8013D2E0`: right code, wrong register and store order).

Tooling bug (not fixed, `tools/` is read-only for this wave): `tools/funcdiff.py` picks the object with `locate(name)`, the first C file that mentions the name. Overlays load at 0x80132000, so a name such as `func_80132B88` exists in several overlays; when another overlay already has the matched C, funcdiff compares that file with its own expected object and prints `MATCH` whatever the target file holds (reproduced with a three-line wrong body in `src/ovl/TT/80132130.c`: `MATCH (relocations resolved)`). Only the sha1 of `ninja` is reliable for such names; `m2c.py` got the unit check in T-3300, `funcdiff.py` should take `--unit` or the INCLUDE_ASM owner the same way.
## Wave 3, list 7 (T-4070): 42 functions in 17 files
Ticket [[tickets/T-4070-wave-3-list-7]]. The list held the functions wave 2 had left over, so most of them are known gaps; 42 of 104 matched (7160 bytes). New patterns:
- **One base symbol stops the load hoist.** IDO hoists the `lbu`/`lh` of a read-modify-write above earlier stores to other globals; the original often did not. When the globals are fields of one record (or neighbours of an array) and the C names them through one symbol, IDO keeps the original order. Matched with it: GYOZI `func_8013683C`, `func_80143D20`, `func_8014569C` (three 0x44-byte records `GyoziRec44 D_8012B8C0[3]` in `include/ovl/GYOZI.h`: `D_8012B8C2` is `[0].unk_02`, `D_8012B94B` is `[2].unk_03`; the struct needs one `s32` member or the copy `rec[2] = rec[0]` becomes `lwl/lwr`), DATE `func_80157FE4` (`D_800E6636[0/2/4]` as an `s16` array through `MAIN_API_OVERRIDE_D_800E6636`), RPG_BAT `func_8013F250` (`D_8015ED8C[2]`, `[1]` is `D_8015ED90`), GEKO `func_80141CCC`, `func_80141E48` (`(&D_8011F4CB)[0x44]` for `D_8011F50F`, `(&D_801217D0)[9]` for `D_801217F4`), OPTION `func_80135034` (`D_80120650[0x553]` etc.: the globals at `D_80120BA3...` are record 20 of the 0x44-byte table). Try this first for any "load placed before the preceding stores" diff.
- **Bit-field flags.** `if (!(D & 0x4000))` compiled as `andi; bnez`, but the original is `sll 17; bltz`, and `D |= 4` at function entry skipped `$t6` (`lbu t7,0(t6)`). Both are bit-field accesses: `typedef struct { unsigned pad:14; unsigned f:1; unsigned rest:17; } BitsB14; if (!((BitsB14 *)&D_800B0B14)->f)` (EVENT `func_80103C3C`) and `((struct { u8 pad:2; u8 f:1; u8 rest:5; } *)&D_800E64B8)->f = 1;` (SHOUGATU `func_801410B4`; the same entry is in BUNKASAI `func_8013B190`, whose remaining difference is the `$v1` selector). These are old T-0018 `regorder` rows and the ENDING "`D & 2` tested as `sll 30; bgez`" case; retry them.
- **A register local declared first moves the next local's slot** (like the T-3330 table idiom): GYOZI `func_80143E08` has `sb t6,0x2B(sp)` for its `u8` flag; `u32 r; u8 flag;` with `r = func_8005B368();` puts the flag at `0x28+3`. A single `u8` alone lands at 0x2F.
- **Four-array setup for `func_8004F870`** (OPTION `func_80134D38`, `func_80134B6C`, `func_80134DD0`, `func_8013539C`, `func_80135034`): `s16 a[2 or 4]..d[]` filled in a short loop, then `func_8004F870(1, n, a, b, c, d)`. The original frame is 8 bytes larger than the arrays need: `s32 i; s32 pad[2];` declared first reproduces frame and offsets (`func_80134D38` has `pad[2]` first and `i` last). That local is a fakematch (`FAKE` comment). The statement order inside the loop decides the pointer registers (`a[i]` before `b[i]` in `func_80134D38`).
- **Fakematches added** (all marked `FAKE`): the `pad` above; `(n ^ 0) != D_800E7389` flips the `beq` operand order (OPTION `func_801324E8`, found by permuter); a repeated `t = t & 0x7F;` (SHUGAKU `func_80132D14`); the no-op `& 0xFF` on two byte stores (main `srn_init`, same trick as `func_8004E44C`).
- **Function-pointer tables** matched with the T-3330 idiom: GYOZI `func_8013DB70`, `func_801447F0`, `func_80143D20`; GEKO `func_8013DCAC`, `func_80141BFC`; SHUGAKU `func_80132274`; DATE2 `func_801326F0`; EVENT `func_80104B50`, `func_801049B4`, `func_80104E64`, `func_80104F80`, `func_801040F0`. The EVENT ones print `"%d\n"` of the index: `idx = D_800B1AF6; func_80078950("%d\n", idx); tbl.f[D_800B1AF6](0x80);` (the second use re-reads the global), which gives the extra word above the table. A table in the main-exe address range that `main_api.h` already declares as another type needs `MAIN_API_OVERRIDE_D_80122EC8` (EVENT). `funcdiff.py` shows only the string relocation as a difference there; ninja's sha1 decides.
- Other matches: constant-pointer initialisers (KANGEI `func_80132090`), unrolled byte loop with an array index (TACO `func_80133694`: `for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) if (D_8015E222[i]) D_8015E222[i]--`), `MoveSquare` caller (main `move_255_line`, implicit declaration), RPG_BAT jump-table switches with equal bodies (`func_8013EEAC`, `func_8013EFD0`: one `case` block per label, no merging).
- **Left as `INCLUDE_ASM`, new gaps.** (a) Loops that IDO unrolls by 4 but with the loads in another rotation: OPTION `func_80134210`, `func_801385F0`, `func_80137FA0` (a `for` over an index starts with element 1; a `while` gives the loads in order but the stores 0,3,2,1; a hand-unrolled body is unrolled again; the end pointer is the symbol `D_80125C50` in the original). TACO `func_80147400` and OPTION `func_80132624` are unrolled by IDO and not by the original. (b) `lw`+`sltiu v1,a0,1` for `D_800F6474++ == 0` needs `D_800F6474` as `u32` (override) and then still differs in the register ($a0): GYOZI `func_8014494C`, `func_8013506C`. (c) Temp numbering that skips two registers (TT `func_80134768`), a shared constant register for `sb` and `sh` of 1 (TT `func_80133C1C`), a dead `li v0,0x60` (OPTION `func_8013357C`). (d) TACO `func_8015D0D0` is gcc/libgte-style code (`addu t7,a0,zero`), not IDO. (e) Delay-slot / sp-adjust placement again: main `func_80054284`, `func_8005448C`, `func_80054704`, `func_80056284` (the `sb` store loop needs the end symbol `D_800E7D1F`); TACO `func_801335A0` (the original duplicates the `andi v0,a1,0xff` join); TACO `func_80147444` (frame +8 and registers `t6/t7`; an unused `RECT` fixes the frame and breaks the temp numbers).
- Tool notes: `funcdiff.py func_X` picked the wrong object for RPG_BAT `func_8013F0F4` (the name also occurs as a call in `8013A650.c`) and reported "missing in built"; `--built <obj>` after `ninja <obj>` is reliable. `--no-build` after a failed ninja compares a stale object and prints MATCH, so read the ninja output first.
## Wave 3 list 10 (T-4100): 115 functions, 21472 bytes
Ticket [[tickets/T-4100-wave-3-list-10]]. Files: main 80062CD0, 80075320, 800737A0; overlays BUNKA_SD, DATE, DATE2, ENDING, ETC, EVENT, GEKO, GYOZI, KANGEI, TACO, TAIIKU (54 of the 131 listed functions, 61 more from the same files). Method: m2c draft, then a scratch driver that rewrites the one function in the real source, compiles it with `tools/cc.py` and diffs it against `expected/` (about 3 s for several variants).

Tooling facts:
- `funcdiff.py --built OBJ --expected OBJ` does not run ninja. Right after an edit it compares the old object, and an all-`INCLUDE_ASM` object is the original, so it printed MATCH for two main functions that did not match (the full build then failed on the sha1). Run `ninja <object>` first, or use a scratch compile.
- With `--resolve`, a call to a renamed callee (`get_g_zyotai_s` for `func_80051A68`) still shows as one `jal` DIFF line; compare the other lines.
- `queue.py --by bytes` over all of my files ended with "error waiting for container: unexpected EOF" after more than 5 minutes; I listed the remaining `INCLUDE_ASM` functions of my files by size from the sources instead.

New patterns that matched:
- **Post-increment compares.** `if (D_800E7380++ >= 1U)` gives `sltiu; xori; addiu; beqz` (`!= 0` gives `sltu` instead), `if (D_800E7384++ >= 0x81U)`, `>= 0x400U` (ENDING `func_801374F8`, KANGEI `func_80136FDC`, BUNKA_SD `func_80138C34`) and `if (D_800E7384++ == 0)` (KANGEI `func_80136238`, `func_80136520`) match. `D++ == 1` followed by a `u16` compare does not (GEKO `func_8013E56C`: the original has `xori; sltiu; beqz`, IDO `xori; bnez`).
- **`s32` function with `return 0;` on the early exits and a fall-off on the main path** gives the `move v0,zero` in the branch delay slots (ENDING `func_8013408C`, `func_80133F98`; `D_800E7384 += 1; if ((u32)D_800E7384 < 2U)`).
- **Function-pointer table, more shapes.** The T-3330 idiom (`s32 idx;` before the table) matches table functions that call `func_80078950("fmt", ...)` between the copy and the index load (EVENT, 9 functions; the format string is a literal and the island of the object provides it), tables copied through a cast of another symbol (`tbl = *(FnTbl32 *)&D_8012277C;` when `main_api.h` has the symbol as `u8`), and tables whose callee gets an argument (0x80). The size is the loop end plus the tail words (one `lw $at` per word; a second `lw $t0,4(..)` is a second word): `0x48 + 4 = 0x4C`, `0x78 + 8 = 0x80`.
- **Local array copied from `.data`** (TAIIKU `func_801470A0`): a struct of three `s32` and `s32 i;` declared before it (`s32 i; Lim3 lim; lim = D_8014A5FC;`) gives the original slot at `sp+0x18`; the other order puts it at `sp+0x1C`.
- **Counted loops over pointer tables** (TACO `func_8014BED8`, `func_8014C8DC`, `func_8014C2E4`, `func_8014D200`, `func_8014C0D4`, `func_8014C1DC`, `func_8014A390`, `func_8014A79C`, `func_8014BE14`): `for (i = 0; i < N; i++) f(i + K, D_table[i], ...)` with `extern s16 *D_table[]` (stride 4). The `bne i,N` or `slti` form of the exit test follows from the code, do not force it. A loop that starts at 1 and passes `i` itself uses `for (i = 1; i < 0x1F; i++) f(i, ..., T[i * 2 - 2], T[i * 2 - 1], B[i * 2 - 2], B[i * 2 - 1])` (TACO `func_8014B374`, `func_8014B4B4`, `func_8014BCEC`, `func_8014B790`).
- **Fields of `TcActor` beyond 0x88 per element:** `D_8015EDB4[16].unk2` is `unk882`; offset 0x84 plus 2 is `unk84[2]`, so `unk98E` is `[17].unk84[2]` and `unk906` is `[16].unk84[2]`.
- **Fixed-point halves:** `D_8014A3DE` is the high half of `D_8014A3DC`. Reading it as `((s16 *)&D_8014A3DC)[1]` keeps the load after the store of the word (TAIIKU `func_80146C20`); the separate symbol hoists it.
- **Strings.** A string passed as `extern` data (`D_800B16F8`, `D_8011FA68`) leaves the symbol undefined at link time once the last asm user is gone: write the literal. A 4-byte zero word in the rodata of the object is an empty string `""` (KANGEI `func_80136EB4`, EVENT `func_8010917C`). `funcdiff.py` reports the string call sites as DIFF (`R_MIPS_HI16 .rodata`); the overlay sha1 decides.
- **`(u8)f() & 0x7F`** (see GYOZI above) also fixes `get_g_zyotai_s(..) & 0x7F` (DATE2 `func_8013780C`, `func_80137948`) and `func_8002328C(..)` (EVENT `func_801087A8`, `func_8010917C`).

Jump-table functions (T-1340 islands) match as written when the selector is a temp: BUNKA_SD `func_80138B3C`, EVENT `func_800FBF40`, `func_801027E0`, `func_801083E0`, TACO `func_80139170`, `func_8014D2E0`, RPG_BAT `func_8014BAA4`, `func_80150AA4`, `func_80150DE0`, `func_80151108`, `func_801515F0`, GYOZI `func_8013829C`, `func_8013A2F4` (nested), ENDING `func_80137AA8`, main `func_80063520`. Write one `case` per table entry with its own `return;` (or `break;` when the original joins), keep a `case N:` that only shares the default (a table that ends one entry short of the original is the sign), and set callees with K&R prototypes. `funcdiff.py` shows only the `jtbl_`/`.rodata` relocation and label lines as differences; the overlay sha1 decides. The functions where the original keeps the selector in $v1 and reloads it (main `func_80077E30`, `func_80075320`, and the 2-4 case compare chains) stay blocked, rows in [[data/t0018-cases]]. `s32 f(void)` without `return` gives `or v0,t6,zero` in the delay slot of the first compare (RPG_BAT `func_801515F0`: `switch (D_8015EDC4 & 0xFFFF0000)` with `*(u8 *)&D_80121603 = 0x80;`, because main_api.h has the byte as `s8` and `li -128` differs).

FAKE, one trick for one gap (17 uses, all `FAKE` comments in the source): when a read-modify-write of a global is followed by loads of globals 4 or 5 bytes further on, IDO hoists the later `lh`/`lbu` above the earlier store and the original does not. Reaching the later fields through the first symbol (`D_800B0A6E += 3; (&D_800B0A6E)[2] += 2; (&D_800B0A6E)[4] -= 0x14;`) keeps the original order (EVENT `func_800FC46C`, `func_800FC994`, `func_80103208`, `func_80102D40`, `func_801030F4`, `func_80109704`, `func_80109A4C`, `func_80109948`, `func_800FCF40`, DATE `func_80159804`, `func_80159A98`, `func_80159BA4`, `func_8015996C`, ENDING `func_801349E0`). It does not help GEKO `func_8013E1A0` and `func_8013B350`. Main `func_80066ACC` needs an empty `if (!ret) {}` (FAKE) to make the result the first register variable ($v1) and the global $a0.

Left `INCLUDE_ASM`, not T-0018:
- Store sunk into the `jal` delay slot: the original keeps `sb`/`sw` in line (GYOZI `func_801381DC`, main `week_day_exit`): IDO moves a store of a read-modify-write that sits right before a call into the delay slot.
- Fptab frame: `func_8013A180` (GEKO, 0x5C table plus a later range test on the same global) comes out 8 bytes too large with the idiom; `func_800FC040` (EVENT) has two scalars above the table but IDO spills one of them; TAIIKU `func_80147120`.
- Struct copy tail: EVENT `func_800FC56C`: the last `lw` of the 0x44-byte copy is scheduled before the `nop` instead of after.
- Shared `lui $at` over several stores, and `$at` sharing (TAIIKU `func_801482BC`, main `menu_bar_color`).
- Redundant range tests in the original (TACO `func_8014D918`, `func_8014E048`, `func_8014C978`, `func_8014E4A4`): the code tests `v < 0x19 || v >= 0x7D` and again `v >= 0x19 && v < 0x7D` in the else branch, and the value sits in $v0; the closest C (`else if` with the second test) has the load in $a0.
- Pointer loop with 16-byte entries and four stores, not unrolled in the original (main `func_800673B8`): `do { p += 0x10; ...} while (p != end)` unrolls with a run-time remainder, the indexed form unrolls by 4.
- `D_800E7384` or `D_80120657` used before and after a call/loop (KANGEI `func_80137544`, `func_80137484`): IDO keeps the `u8` in a register across the loop.
- GEKO fade loops with `u8 D_800B593C` thresholds (`func_80139D54`, `func_8013E69C`, `func_8013E760`, `func_80139C70`): base, mask and counter registers are assigned in another order.
- T-0018 selectors: rows in [[data/t0018-cases]] (ETC dispatchers, GEKO `func_8013B030`, main dispatchers).
## Shared constants, shared `lui $at` and loop unrolling (T-3001, T-5020)
Tickets [[tickets/T-3001-shared-constant-registers]], [[tickets/T-5020-loop-unrolling-and-lui-sharing]]. Three blockers that waves 2 and 3 reported often. Each was measured over every function file of the main exe and the 26 overlays (`asm/**/{matchings,nonmatchings}`, SDK files excluded) with scratch scripts (not committed), and tested against IDO 5.3 by running cfe, uopt, ugen and as1 by hand with every option of interest. Verdicts: (a) and (b) are toolchain differences with no C idiom and no IDO option; (c) is mostly a source difference, with one toolchain part.

### (a) Constants reused across stores: toolchain (uopt), no fix
- The original usually re-materialises a constant per store, like IDO with `-Wo,-nokpicopt`: in straight-line stores of the same constant to globals there are 1052 re-loads and 124 reuses (an earlier register still holding the value) in the original. Stock IDO (without `-nokpicopt`) reuses every time, in `$v0/$v1`. So `-nokpicopt` stays right for about 90% of the sites; the 124 reuses (DATE `func_8013DCC0`, `func_8013BEB8`, EVENT `func_80118868`, `func_80119C9C`, GYOZI `func_8014210C`, ...) are allocation decisions of the original's uopt, made in `$t6/$t7/$t8` or `$t2/$t3` and sometimes hoisted above a branch.
- The same function can have both: `func_800419FC` re-loads `li 0x28` four times and `li 1` eight times between stores to neighbouring globals, while DATE `func_8013DCC0` reuses 3, 4 and 2. No property of the source found so far separates them (same store widths, same block structure, no call in the live range in either).
- Not reproduced by: a local (`s16 t = 3`), a `register` local, chain assignment (`A = B = 3`), and every uopt option in IDO 5.3's option table (`-kpicopt` after `-nokpicopt`, `-no_const_in_reg`, `-docopy`, `-doassoc`, `-moremotion`, `-zmovc:0/1`, `-zcopy:0`, `-zaloc`, `-zstor`, `-regr 8`, `-rege 4`, ... 40 settings): IDO with `-nokpicopt` emits one `li` per store in every case. The constants are `LDC J` in cfe's ucode already, so type unification (the T-3001 note on TT `func_80149108`) only helps loop constants.
- Update (T-9020): the reuses are chain assignments whose targets are array or record elements (`D_800CA224[0] = D_800CA224[1] = D_800CA224[2] = 3;`); with a scalar anywhere in the chain IDO re-loads per store. See "Wave-5 codegen shapes (T-9020)".
- The `li at,1` per compare of the EVENT/DATE compare chains is the opposite case (the original re-loads, IDO shares in `$v0`); the `li 0x38; multu` of two straight-line `idx * 0x38` is the same family (a constant the original keeps in a register in straight-line code). All stay `INCLUDE_ASM`; rows in [[data/t0018-cases]].

### (b) One `lui $at` for several stores: as1 rule plus how the source names the data, no adoptable pass
- **Corrected by T-9010** (section "Shared `lui $at`: data defined in the file" below): IDO 5.3 shares `$at` for one symbol defined in the same file; the measurement here used externs. No pass is needed.
- IDO 5.3's as1 reuses `$at` only for a store to exactly the same symbol and offset, within a basic block, until something else writes `$at` (measured: `VA = n; VA = v;` shares, `S.a = n; S.b = v;` and `A[0] = n; A[1] = v;` do not, for externs, statics, volatile, as1 `-O0` to `-O3`; stock `-O2`/`-O3` put the base in `$v0` instead). No C form gives one `lui $at` for two different offsets.
- The original shares `$at` across different offsets in 171 groups (all stores; 74 functions, all still `INCLUDE_ASM`), and re-emits `lui $at` in 19026 places where the next store has the same `%hi` (mostly neighbours 1 to 8 bytes apart). Per original object (`config/objects/*.txt`), no address pair is shared in one place and re-emitted in another: 320 shared pairs, 10669 re-emitted pairs, 0 conflicts. Across objects there are conflicts (dec_bg_reset shares `D_800B5920/24`, DATE re-emits them; RPG_BAT `func_8014E994` against `func_8014C360`), which fits declarations per source file.
- Reading: the original's as1 keyed the `$at` reuse on the symbol (the `lui` instruction word with its HI16 relocation, which is the same for `sym` and `sym+2`), not on symbol plus offset, and the source reached the shared groups through one symbol (struct or array) and the re-emitted ones through separate symbols. RPG_BAT `func_8014E994` shows the boundaries: `D_8015EE60/64` shared, `EE68/6C` shared, `EE70` alone, `EE74/78/7C` shared. The groups are in [[data/shared-at-groups]] (for type recovery).
- Why no pass: an as1 rule "reuse `$at` for the same symbol" would change 135 matched C functions that reach neighbouring globals through one base symbol (`D_80120650[3]`, `(&D_800E65FE)[2]`, `RpgStat`, ...) while the original re-emits `lui $at` there. Under the reading above those were separate symbols in the original, and the one-symbol C is there to stop IDO's as1 from hoisting a load above a store, which the original's as1 did not do. Both as1 rules (symbol-keyed `$at` reuse and the load-hoisting alias rule) would have to be modelled together, and the hoisting rule is not known (the original hoists in 91 of 680 read-modify-write pairs on separate symbols). Struct recovery alone does not fix (b): IDO re-emits `lui $at` for struct fields.

### (c) Loop unrolling: mostly the source
IDO 5.3's uopt unrolls a loop by 4 (`-loopunroll`, default 4), by 2 when 4 copies exceed `-unrolllimit` (a size budget, default between 250 and 341 units; `-unrolllimit 0` turns unrolling off), and handles an unknown trip count with a remainder loop. A uniform flag does not model the original: a full rebuild with `-Wo,-unrolllimit,N` changes 8 matched functions at N = 60 to 80, 6 at 100, 4 at 150 and none at 200 or 250, and `-Wo,-nomultibbunroll` changes 8 (multi-block bodies the original does unroll), while the blocked loops below have small bodies that any such N still unrolls. What decides is how the loop is written:
- **Struct-array element fields keep a big body from being unrolled.** Main `func_800415B4` (20 stores per 36-byte entry, run-time bounds): `((SprEnt *)&D_801217D0)[i].unk_04 = 0;` per field gives the original's single loop, because uopt counts each struct-array access as more units (342 against the default budget). The pointer form (`SprEnt *e = ...; e->unk_04 = 0`) and the byte-offset casts (`*(s16 *)(p + 4)`) count less and get unrolled by 4. Matched.
- **A signed counter against an unsigned bound is not unrolled.** TACO `func_80147400` (copy loop, `sltu`): `s32 i; u32 n = *(u32 *)(arg0 + 8); for (i = 1; i < n + 1; i++)` keeps the original's plain loop, because uopt cannot compute the trip count of a mixed-signedness compare. `u32 i` (or `s32 n`) is unrolled with a remainder loop. Matched.
- **A pointer loop whose end is the start symbol plus a constant is not unrolled** (`for (p = D_800E6280; p != D_800E6280 + 0x200; p += 0x10)`, also `p < D + N`). It gives the original's loop shape for main `func_800673B8`, NAME_ENT `func_801478E0` and the first loop of BUNKA_SD `func_8013987C`/`func_80134418`; the end pointer is then recomputed in the loop (`addiu t7,v1,0x200`) where the original holds `&D_800E6480` in a register, so these stay `INCLUDE_ASM`. An index loop over the same range is unrolled by 4 (constant trip count).
- **Toolchain part: the original never unrolls a pointer loop with an unknown trip count.** IDO unrolls `p != &OTHER_SYMBOL` loops with a run-time remainder (`subu; andi 0x3F; beqz` before a remainder loop); the original has no such prologue in any of its 6958 functions (scan for `subu` followed by `andi` with a power-of-two mask: 12 hits, all byte arithmetic), while it does use the integer-counter remainder (`andi x,n,3; beqz`, 72 sites, e.g. `k_reset`). No IDO option switches only that off. ETC `func_80142AE8` and main `func_80071038` belong here (plus the unfilled `lbu; nop` delay slots of `func_80142AE8`, an as1 difference).
- Not solved: OPTION `func_80132624` (8 iterations, two if/else in the body): IDO unrolls it by 2 at any budget between 144 and 287 and by 4 above, the original not at all; with the 200 that the matched loops need it would be unrolled by 2. Either the original never unrolls by 2 or the body was written differently; open. The OPTION rotation cases (`func_80134210`, `func_801385F0`, `func_80137FA0`) were not retried.
- Update (T-7020): the toolchain part and the open cases are source forms after all. OPTION `func_80132624` is unrolled by 2 in the original too, TT `func_80142D40` is a 0x20-step loop that both compilers unroll, the "hoisted end pointer" is a rolled index loop (nested loop or a counter used again later), and the OPTION rotation cases are one-line loops. See "Loop unrolling and scheduling (T-7020)".

## Recovered data types replace the "first symbol" trick (T-5000)

- The "load hoisted above an earlier store" family is a type problem: the original reached those globals through one struct or array symbol, so it kept the load after the store; IDO does the same once the C declares the aggregate. 33 `FAKE` index tricks were replaced by real types with the same bytes (`Rec34`, `Rec38`, `Rec24`, `Work80125D10`, `RpgRec18`, and the base `D_8011ECD0` / `D_800EAFA0` of the 0x44-byte record tables); 7 load-hoist functions left as asm now match (GYOZI `func_80135C3C`, KANGEI `func_80135C18`, SHUGAKU `func_80135B68`, NAME_ENT `func_8013B51C`, EVENT `func_800F87BC`, DATE `func_8013C700`, `func_8013D9E8`). Method, counts, and the 20 functions retried: [[data-types]]. Tool: `tools/type_recovery.py --sym D_X`.
- Struct, not array, when the fields are summed: RPG_BAT `func_801516E4` matches with `RpgRec18` and not with `s32[6]` (IDO orders the operands of array elements differently).
- The base symbol is the one the original relocations use: `Rec24 D_801217D0[]` matches, `Rec24 D_801217AC[]` with `&T[1]` does not (IDO folds the element offset into the load offsets). splat's `D_801217AC` is entry `i - 1`.
- A flag word read as a value and tested twice (`lw` once, two `sll; bltz`) is `D[i].flags.b12 || D[i].flags.b13` with a bit-field member; a pointer to the member reloads the word (EVENT `func_8011C514`).
- The original sign-extends an `s16` argument in the callee (`sll/sra` of `$a0`); IDO 5.3 does not, with an ANSI or a K&R `s16` parameter (DATE `func_80148924`, main `func_80079F00`).
- Still open: the main bss block `0x800E6248..0x800E7400` behaves as one object (SHUGAKU `func_80135994`, GEKO `func_8013B350` match only when `D_800E69A0` and `D_800E7388`, 0x9E8 apart, go through one symbol), and the full field layout of the 0x44-byte record table.

## Game-state struct (T-5100)

- The main bss block 0x800E6280..0x800E7D10 is `GameState D_800E6280` ([[game-state]]). The base comes from the original's strength-reduced loops (0x800E6280 in the base register, the arrays at large offsets), not from the first accessed label. All 1459 C uses were rewritten to field accesses with `tools/migrate_globals.py`, byte-identically; one use stays on its old name (SHOUGATU `func_8013E0F0`, `config/migrate_globals.txt`).
- 47 functions whose original keeps a load of the block after a store to another of its fields now match from plain m2c C (191 tried); none of them matches with separate extern symbols. 7 "first symbol" `FAKE` tricks are gone.
- IDO differences found on the way, valid for any aggregate: a constant-index array element and a struct member differ in a `switch` (register of the selector) and in a sum (load order); `((u8 *)&S)[k + i]` differs from the old `u8 S[]` view in the induction variable; a struct-array element in a sum can load in the other order than a scalar extern ([[data-types]], "Aggregates and old names").
- Near misses left as asm: KANGEI `func_80134EA8` (the original's local sits 4 bytes lower in the frame), DATE `func_801573F0`, `func_80145DF0` and 9 others with temp-register order differences (T-0018 family), and the larger ones that need hand work (`check_para_limit`, DATE `func_80139944`, ...). U0 selectors are not changed by the struct (ETC `func_80145960`).

## Wave 4 list 2 (T-6020): 13 functions, 2580 bytes, 34 files

13 of the 151 listed functions match (about 1 in 12 tried; many of the rest are listed near-miss shapes). Matched: TACO `func_801368D8`, `func_801322AC`; main `func_80062C28`; SHUGAKU `func_8013A274`; DATE2 `func_80132EC8`; NAME_ENT `func_80140ED4`, `func_80145454`, `func_80140BA4`, `func_8014AE2C`, `func_80145760`, `func_80147950`, `func_8014AD44`; EVENT `func_800F7F44`. New or confirmed patterns:
- **Post-increment compare is the old value.** m2c prints `D += 1; if (D >= N)` for `lbu/lw; sltiu|slti; addiu; sb|sw`, but the original compares the old value: `if (D_800E6280.unk_110D++ >= 0x41U)` (NAME_ENT `func_80140BA4`) and `if (D_8014D184++ >= 0x3D)` (`func_8014AD44`). This differs from the `D++ == 1` case ([[data/t0018-cases]], GEKO `func_80135A6C`), where the original materialises `(old ^ 1) < 1` and stays asm.
- **State-machine switches on a `u8` GameState field match** when the cases only call functions or add 1 (`func_80140ED4`, `func_80145454`, `func_8014AE2C`, `func_80145760`); the T-0018 shape is the same switch with a compare chain or a `move v0,v1` of the selector (TACO `func_8013587C`, NAME_ENT `func_80145378`, main `func_800626B0`, rows in the data file).
- **Unrolled struct-array stores keep the table shape**: TACO `func_801322AC` is `D_8015EDB4[0]` and `D_8015EDB4[8]` (the second actor is 8 records of 0x88 away); m2c prints the second as `D_8015EDB4->unk4xx`. Writing it as `[8].field` matched at once.
- **A table of fields 4 bytes apart is a record array**: EVENT `func_800F7F44` matches with `D_800B0A04[8].unk_02/unk_06/unk_0A` (the `Rec34` table) instead of three separate globals; with separate globals IDO hoists the loads. `D_801207B0` is stored as `sh` there, so the s32 symbol needs `*(s16 *)&D_801207B0 = 3`.
- **`u8` parameter with a modulo**: TACO `func_801368D8(u8 *arg0, u8 arg1)` with `((u32)v + arg1 + 1) % arg1` and `u8 v` (the cast order matters: `(v + arg1 + 1U)` gives `addu a1,v0`, the original `addu v0,a1`).
- **Spill slot one word lower than IDO gives**: NAME_ENT `func_80147950` needs one unused 4-byte local declared before the temp that is spilled across the calls (`FAKE` comment in the source); same shape as the KANGEI `func_80134EA8` near miss of T-5100.
- **Not matched, new shapes** (left as `INCLUDE_ASM`): (a) `D_x = 7; f(7)` where the original shares one `li a0,7` between the store and the argument (GEKO `func_8013CC20`, GYOZI `func_8013D764`; the same family as the shared constants in "Shared constants, shared `lui $at`"); (b) a `lui` of the second global hoisted by IDO into the delay slot of the first `bnez` while the original leaves a `nop` (RPG_BAT `func_8013C1CC`), or a delay slot that the original fills from the branch target (`lw ra`, SHUGAKU `func_8013A980`; `li a0,0x41`, NAME_ENT `func_8014A848`); (c) address CSE: `(arg0 * 0x1C) + D_8014A454` computed once by IDO and twice by the original (TAIIKU `func_80147A6C`, `func_801482BC`); (d) four `s16` globals decremented in a row, loads next to the stores in the original and hoisted by IDO (TAIIKU `func_801487B4`); (e) an unrolled-by-4 loop whose loads start with the first element in the original and the fourth in IDO (TACO `func_80133410`, 4 instructions off; `func_801334BC` differs only in the place of one `sw zero`); (f) `x = f() & 0x7F; if (x == 2) ... else if (x == 3)`: the original keeps the call result in a copy (`move v1,v0; andi t6,v1,0x7f`), GEKO `func_80136F20` and `func_80137040`; (g) `func_80141FA4`-style functions that return the result of void callees (NAME_ENT `func_80141FA4`, TACO `func_80138890`): the callee definitions are `void`, the original reads `$v0`; (h) NAME_ENT `func_80147F78` family (three twins `func_801469D0`, `func_80147A80`): the original keeps `var_a0` in `$a0` and the frame is 8 bytes bigger than IDO's; (i) TT `func_8013BE9C`: `D_80158A90` goes to `$a2` and the argument to `$a3` in the original, the reverse in IDO.
- Process lesson: `expected/` objects must be copied from a build made before ANY edit; a copy taken after the first edit made `funcdiff.py` report MATCH for a function that did not match (caught by the sha1 of the overlay).
## Wave 4, list 6 (T-6060): 150 functions in 33 files, main exe and 14 overlays
Ticket [[tickets/T-6060-wave-4-list-6]]. 40 functions, 6724 bytes matched (39 from the list, 6564 bytes, plus EVENT `func_80101510`; verified by the 27 sha1 checks of a clean build); 17 rows added to [[data/t0018-cases]]. Patterns that worked:
- A `switch` with a jump table or a long compare chain is the first thing to try for every m2c output with `goto block_N` or `/* irregular */`: `func_80116EF0` (EVENT) has gotos in m2c and is `switch (D) { case 5: case 9: ...; break; case 7: ...; default: ... }`; `func_80075468` is `case 0: default: A; break; case 1: B; break;` (the shared default block follows the compare chain); `func_801446F8` (RPG_BAT), `func_80101510` (EVENT), `func_8013C818` (TT) match as plain switches whose table IDO emits itself (funcdiff shows only the table relocations, the overlay sha1 decides). A `u8` selector argument is declared `u8 arg0` (`func_8013C818`), not masked inside.
- Dispatcher with a local copy of a function-pointer table (`memcpy` in m2c): `FnTblN tbl; tbl = D_x; ...; tbl.f[D_800E6280.unk_110A](0x80);` with an unused `s32 idx;` declared first for the frame offset (T-3330, marked `FAKE`): DATE `func_8015908C`, `func_801591BC`, `func_8014FBA0`; ENDING `func_80133540`, `func_80133AD0`. The index is the global itself, loaded again after the call that precedes it (a local `idx = D` is CSE'd and misses). The table size is the copied byte count (a trailing 8-byte move adds 2 entries). EVENT `func_80102118` needs the table 4 bytes lower and is open.
- Implicit-int, no `return` (T-1321 batch guidance): `s32 holiday_club(void)` with a `switch` and no return gives the `or v0,v1,zero` copy; a function that returns a value only on some paths and falls off the end otherwise (`s32 func_80135A80(void)`, `func_801338F8`: `return 0;` after a call in the early branch, nothing at the end) gets the leftover `$v0` the original has. `s32` return with several `return 0` paths that the original shares: write one final `return 0;` and let the other arms fall out (`func_8006CDD4`: `if (a == 1) { if ... else if ... } else if (a == 0) { ...; return 1; } return 0;`).
- Post-increment compare: `if (D++ >= 0x400U)` (GEKO `func_80142A5C`) compares the old value as the original does; `D += 1; if (D >= ...)` does not.
- Unsigned compare to unshare constants: a chain of `== 1` tests on `u8`/`s32` globals in which the original loads `li at,1` before every test (IDO shares one register): cast the global, `(u32)D_800EECBC == 1`, `(u32)(u8)D_80125320 == 3`, `(u32)D_800B3CFC == 1U` (EVENT `func_8011A508`, `func_80119824`; ETC `func_80140AE4`). `(u32)D_800E6280.unk_75D > 0` gives the original `sltiu; xori 1` for `x != 0` stored to a `s16` (OMIMAI `func_801327BC`).
- `s32` selector in `$v1`: the file declares `extern u32 D_80122CD0;` after `#define MAIN_API_OVERRIDE_D_80122CD0` and the wrapper in `main_api.h` (DATE `func_8014F370`); a `(u32)` cast in the `switch` does not change the register.
- A pointer loop whose pointer is also the loop variable: `r += 0x12C; for (i = 3; i < 0x1F; i++) { *r = 0; r += 0x64; }` after three explicit stores gives the original's in-place `addiu v0,v0,0xDAC` pointer (TT `func_801426F0`); indexing `r[i * 0x64]` allocates a new pointer register.
- Array loop over records with a struct array: `D_800E6280.unk_1BC[i].unk_02` for a 11-trip loop keeps the displacement and strides the base (`func_80071038`); `for (i = 0; i != 0xE; i++)` with `icon_can_use_set` (`func_8006CE84`).
- Stack structs the headers do not have (DRAWENV 0x60 bytes, DISPENV 0x14): a file-local struct with the leading `RECT` and a filler array reproduces the frame (`func_800410AC`); the SDK prototypes it passes them to are K&R `()` in `main_api.h`.
- Rec34 (`D_800B0A04[]`) gets `unk_27` (split from `pad23`) for EVENT `func_8011552C`; `D_800B0A04[1].unk_02` is `D_800B0A3A`.
- Byte-offset style on the TT pointer globals (`u8 *p = D_80158A8C; *(s16 *)(p + 0x256) = ...`): `func_8013ABB0` needs the table read (`p[0xCA] = D_80155A8C[i]`) placed second in the source; `func_8013ADC0` needs the reload of `+0x26` as the first statement of the then-block; the TT mode switches `func_8013CB58`/`func_8013D060` are `switch (*(u16 *)(p + 4))`.

Left `INCLUDE_ASM` (new shapes; 17 are rows in [[data/t0018-cases]]):
- Stack `RECT` filled x,y,w,h and passed to `func_8009C7F8`: the original stores `h` before `w`; all 24 statement orders give `w` first (`func_8004111C`, `func_80059308`).
- `return 0;` as the last statement after stores through `lui at`: the original puts `or v0,zero,zero` before the last `lui at; sb` and the store in the delay slot, IDO the `move v0,zero` (`func_80042808`, `func_80042908`, `func_8004284C`, `func_80042940`).
- One-higher ugen temp numbering after a call-result use (ETC `func_80140AE4`, EVENT `func_801146B4`; TT `func_8013C818` showed it with an `s32` argument and matched after the parameter became `u8`).
- Constant shared in a register where the original loads it per use, or the other way round (TT `func_8013D150` `0x30000`, SHUGAKU `func_80135DB8` `56` shared with the call argument, EVENT `func_80118868` ten stores of 2/3, `func_80119440`, SHUGAKU `func_80138DD8`).
- Saved-register assignment of loops (TACO `func_8015435C`, `func_80156244`, `func_80155230`: pointers to `D_8015EDB4[arg0]` reloaded per group; the original puts the pointer in `$a1` and the switch value in `$v0`).
- Loop pointer/counter swap (TT `func_80146BE0`, `func_80146C14`: pointer `$v1`, counter `$v0`; main `func_8004164C`: end pointer `$a2`, data pointer `$a3`, exact otherwise; `initModelingData_init`: copy of `arg0 + 4` into `$s2` before the add).
- GameState word view of a `u8[4]` member (main `func_800722C4`): the original reads `D_800E71E4` as a word through its own symbol; through `unk_F64` the word and the bytes share one base register. A `u32` member at +0xF64 or an exception in `config/migrate_globals.txt` would fix it.
- A call that the old header gives fewer arguments than the callers pass (`func_8009B430`: 3 or 4, `func_80053650`: defined `void(s32,s32,s32)`, called with 4) blocks `initModelingData_init` and `func_8005751C`.

Tooling notes (not edited): `tools/permute.py` printed score 0 for main `func_8004111C`, TT `func_8013C764` and EVENT `func_8011A508`; for the last one the permuter's winning source differs from `base.c` in an extern type (`D_800EECBC` became `unsigned long`, which was the real fix), for the first two the source is identical to the C that ninja builds with another result. Diff the winning `source.c` declarations against the real headers before trusting a score 0. `funcdiff.py` compares against `expected/`: copy the pristine objects before the first edit; an `expected/` object copied after a build of edited C makes every later MATCH of that file meaningless (found only through the overlay sha1).
## Wave 4, list 3 (T-6030): 19 functions, 3384 bytes
Ticket [[tickets/T-6030-wave-4-list-3]]. 19 of the 144 listed functions match (main `800737A0`/`800674B0`, EVENT, KANGEI, OPTION, RPG_BAT, SHOUGATU, TACO). The list is the hard remainder (most are register-order near misses); the idioms below are what turned near misses into matches.
- Read a global back through the global after an in-place update, not through a temporary. `D_8015EDB4 += 4; f(0xF0 - D_8015EDB4)` and `D_8015EC50 = a - b; ... D_8015EC50 * D_8015EE10 / 1000` match (RPG_BAT `func_80132CE4`, `func_80132DE8`, `func_80143DA8`); the m2c form with `temp_v0 = D + 4; D = temp_v0; f(.. temp_v0)` gets another register (`$a3` for `$v0`). IDO forwards the stored value, and a named temporary changes the allocation.
- The value of a post-incremented counter is compared before the increment: `if (D_800E6280.unk_1104.u++ >= 0x400U)` (SHOUGATU `func_80137940`) gives `sltiu; xori 1; ...; addiu; sw` with the load in `$v1`. m2c shows `D += 1; if (D >= ...)`, which is the other order. The `==` form (`u++ == 0x3C`, SHUGAKU `func_80134024`) stays one register off (`sltiu $t6` for `sltiu $v0`).
- A switch function whose cases only call and have no `default` return: the original is non-void, so the delay slot of the first `beqz` carries `or $v0,$v1,$zero` (the selector, never returned on purpose). Declare it `u8` and write no `return` (main `vacation_day_init`, TACO `func_80142F34`, RPG_BAT `func_8013D780`; the same for an `s16` result of `D += 1` as the last statement, main `func_8006C934`). `void` plus `return D` in a default case gives another epilogue layout.
- `tools/queue.py` T hint on a global switch: EVENT `func_800FD360` matches with `extern u32 D_800EECB0` (`#define MAIN_API_OVERRIDE_D_800EECB0` before the includes, the symbol wrapped in `include/main_api.h`). A `(u32)` cast in the switch does not do it. The same declaration change did not help RPG_BAT `func_80136A9C` (selector read after a branch: row in [[data/t0018-cases]]).
- A call whose arguments are not on the stack but which gets no argument at all: m2c prints `func_80084D3C(0x38)` when `$a0` still holds the multiplier of a `Rec38` index; the original passes nothing (KANGEI `func_80138470`). Check the `jal` for `li $a0` in its own delay slot before believing an m2c argument.
- `x * 0x38` with `multu` and the constant in `$a0` appears when the index is a `u8` (KANGEI, `unk_1BC[D_800E71DF]`) but not for an `s32`/`u32` global index (SHOUGATU `func_80134730`: `sll; subu; sll`).
- A bit test of one flag of a word (`sll 30; bgez`/`srl 31`) is a local bit-field view, LSB first: `typedef struct { u32 pad0 : 1; u32 flag : 1; u32 rest : 30; }` (TACO `func_80142DE0` on `D_800E7D50`, KANGEI `func_8013614C` on `unk_1BC[i].unk_0C`). `(u32) x >> 4` for a byte (`srl`), not `(u8) x >> 4` (`sra`).
- A pointer kept in a stack slot with an unused 4-byte slot above it (ENDING `func_80139B48` did not match for registers; KANGEI `func_80136810` matches): declare `s32 unused; /* FAKE */` first, as for the local function-pointer tables (T-3330). A local table copy whose index is read straight from the global (DATE `func_80154E48`, `func_80155004`) needs the same unused scalar first, then the frame is right.
- A repeated sub-expression (`D_80139DF4[(u8)D_80139DF0]` three times) is one value in `$v1` in the original; a named local is allocated differently (`$a0`), the bare expression repeated three times is right for KANGEI `func_8013614C`.
- Not solved (kept as `INCLUDE_ASM`; the best attempts are not in the tree):
  - A `GameState` member where the original has a separate scalar (DATE `func_8015522C`: `D_800E6481 |= 0x40`; main `get_weekly_bg_sector`: `D_800E691D[D_800E699C * 4]`): the original takes `lui`/`%lo` of the scalar itself (two `lui $at`, no shared base), the member form folds the offset and shares the base register. Only a `keep` line in `config/migrate_globals.txt` would allow the scalar; the brief forbids that file. (T-7010: wrong diagnosis. The scalar gives the same words; `func_8015522C` is a bit-field store and matched, `get_weekly_bg_sector` is register allocation. See "Game-state views audit (T-7010)".)
  - `x = x | 3` on a `u8` global with `andi $v0,$t7,0xFF` before the `sb` (main `func_80067DD4`, `func_80067DFC`): IDO drops the mask; decomp-permuter best score 20.
  - Six 0x68-byte records stored through a `u8 *` global read once into `$v0` with `addiu $v0,0x208` (TT `func_801473BC`, `func_801473E8`): IDO reloads the global or takes `$t6`.
  - Constant registers: TACO `func_801421AC`/`func_801420DC` (`beq $a0,$v1` with the constants `3`, `4` in `$a0`/`$a1` and the value in `$v1`), TT `func_801475C8` (loop bound `6` in `$s2`, key `0x40` in `$s3`; IDO swaps them), main `strSetDefDecEnv` (`$v0 = 0x140`, `$v1 = 0xF0` hoisted; store order of three pairs).
  - Register naming only (one temp off): TT `func_8013923C`, `func_80133058`, ENDING `func_80139B48`/`func_80139EC4` (the loaded byte goes to `$a1`, the argument register of the later call), KANGEI `func_80136970` (`$v1` and `$a0` swapped), SHOUGATU `func_801341E4`/`func_801345C8`/`func_80134648`/`func_8013BD2C` (`func_80051A68() & 0x7F` is a named variable copied through `$v1` in the original), OPTION `func_80139F04`/`func_80139F8C`/`func_80139878`.
  - A `(u8 value != 0)` used as a number: the original has `sltiu t,v,1; xori t,t,1` in place, IDO emits `sltu t,$zero,v`; `(v == 0) ^ 1` gives the original pair on a fresh register, which moves every later temporary by one (GYOZI `func_80140330`, SHOUGATU `func_8013BC2C`). `!!x`, `!(x == 0)` and a local copy do not help.
  - m2c, `sp2B`-style locals and `goto`s of TACO `func_8014D918`/`func_8014E048`: the structure is easy, the register of the byte (`$v0` for `$a0`) is not.
## Wave 4 list 1 (T-6010): 17 functions, 3644 bytes, 35 owned files
Matched (all in the owned files): DATE `func_8015A3B0`, `func_8015A414`; TEL `func_80135FFC`, `func_801360E4`; TT `func_80148974`, `func_8013EFAC`, `func_801364EC`, `func_80135E9C`, `func_80132000`; SHOUGATU `func_80138868`, `func_801388F0`, `func_80138964`; EVENT `func_800FAE6C`, `func_800FB188`; TACO `func_80151C30`, `func_801534E0`; GYOZI `func_80142E50`. `func_801360E4` is not on the list: it is the byte-identical twin of `func_80135FFC` that `queue.py --by bytes` showed (`dupes.py --apply` did not fill it, so the C was copied by hand).

New patterns:
- **Flag set on a byte is a bit-field store, not `|=`.** `D.b[0] |= 4` on a `Rec38` flag byte gives one temporary too many (`lbu t9` where the original has `lbu t0`); `((Bits *)&x)->f = 1` with `typedef struct { u8 pad:2; u8 f:1; u8 rest:5; } Bits;` gives the original's temporaries (SHOUGATU `func_80138868`, `func_801388F0`, `func_80138964`, the shape already used in `8013F190.c`; GYOZI `func_80142E50` with `u8 b0:1; u8 b1:1` for `unk_10[0]`). LSB first: `pad:2; f:1` is bit 2 (value 4). The same word can be tested through a 32-bit bit-field (`((GyoziBits32 *)girl[9].unk_10)->b1` is `sll 30; bltz`) while the byte is set through an 8-bit one.
- **A 0x44-byte record copy needs a word-aligned type.** `Ev44 { s32 w[0x11]; }` gives the 3-word `lw/sw` loop plus the 2-word tail; `u8 b[0x44]` compiles to `lwl/lwr/swl/swr` (EVENT `func_800FAE6C`, `*(Ev44 *)&D_800EAFA0[0x88] = *(Ev44 *)D_800EAFA0`).
- **`*arg++` as the last call argument puts the increment into the `jal` delay slot** (TT `func_80148974`: `func_801487D0(arg0, arg1, *arg2++)` inside `while (*arg2 != 0)`; `c = *arg2; arg2++; call(c)` leaves the `addiu` before the arguments).
- **A block shared by three paths is written with `goto`**, the data pointer is an expression (`*(u8 **)arg0`, not a local), and the sum is index first (`arg0[6] * 3 + *(u8 **)arg0`) to get `addu v0,v1,t7` (TT `func_80132000`). Storing `arg0[9] = *e` before `arg0[8] |= 1` lets as1 hoist the load of `*e` above the flag store, as the original has it.
- **A `u16` field compared or shifted must be unsigned in C**: `>= 0x1A4U`, `(u32)*(u16 *)p >> 4` (`srl`), a `u16 += 8; if (>= 0x2001U) -= 0x2000` pair (TT `func_80135E9C`, `func_8013A040`).
- **A `lw` selector of a shared main global needs the `u32` view** (DATE `func_8015A3B0`, `MAIN_API_OVERRIDE_D_80122CD0`): `$v1` instead of `$v0`. The cast `(u32)D` in the `switch` does not do it.
- **Unused slots above a local are FAKE padding**: TT `func_8013EFAC` (`s32 pad;` above the pointer local, 4 bytes) and TACO `func_80151C30` (`s32 pad[4]`, 16 bytes) match only with them. Their real source is unknown (a struct or RECT local the original never touches).
- **`s16` store of a constant that is also a `case` label shares the register**: `*(s16 *)(p + 4) = 0x40` shares the loop-hoisted `0x40` of the compare chain, `*(u16 *)` does not (TT `func_80149108`, not matched for another reason, below).
- **Overlay headers lie about some widths**: ENDING `D_8013C35C` is declared `s32`, every use in the asm is `lh/sh`; the type was not changed (`s16` would be right for the three ENDING functions that read it, which stay asm for other reasons).

Not matched, by cause (all reverted to `INCLUDE_ASM`):
- **Second read of a `u8` global as an index after a call is `lbu $v1`** in the original and `$t4` in IDO (EVENT `func_800FA6A0`, GYOZI `func_80142D40`; the T-3330 table idiom is right, only that register differs). Rows in [[data/t0018-cases]]. A `u8` global read after a call and kept across a loop is `$a1` (EVENT `func_800FA194`, `func_800FA0D0`).
- **State machine on `u8 D_800E738D` returning `D += 1`**: the original keeps selector, reloads and the result in `$v0`, IDO uses `$v1` (BUNKA_SD `func_80134314`, with the old scalar name and with a local copy as well).
- **A `u8` parameter converted at entry** (`andi t6,a0,0xff; or a0,t6,zero`; main `func_80045318`) and a modified `u8` parameter kept in its home slot (TT `func_80148B6C`) are the known pattern of `func_80132048`.
- **Shared constants**: `li a1,1` reused by `sh` and `sb` stores (TT `func_8013A040`, `func_8013ED70`), the stride constant 0x38 shared with the call argument (SHOUGATU `func_80142784`), the `D_8015EB30` address CSE'd into `$a0` for two loops where the original re-materialises it (TACO `func_8013DDE4`).
- **Stack argument stored before the register arguments** (`sw zero,16(sp)` ahead of the `move a0`) where the original has it in the delay slot (TACO `func_80152110`).
- **Original copies the incoming `arg0` to `$a3` and spills four registers around the call**; IDO keeps `arg0` in its home slot and gives `$a3` to another variable (TACO `func_80141050`, `func_80141170`, `func_80141290`).
- **Three adjacent `s16` read-modify-writes on 0x44-byte records** (TAIIKU `func_80140338`, `func_8014038C`): the original reloads each `lh` after the previous `sh`, IDO hoists the loads; a base+index form changes the `lui` words. The TAIIKU state block at `D_80149940` is an array of `GsWord f[3]` records (stores `r[8].f[0].h[1]`, ... give the right offsets), but the stores share one `lui at` in the original ([[data/shared-at-groups]]), so `func_8013ACBC` and the other group members stay asm.
- **Loop with a second pointer bound** (`p != D_80121750`, BUNKA_SD `func_80134418`): unrolled with a run-time remainder.
- **Temp numbering or register order one off** (DATE `func_80149F48`, `func_80149AC0`, `func_80145DF0`; TT `func_8013EC64`, `func_80149400`, `func_8013ED70`; TAIIKU `func_8013C694`, `func_80140738`; EVENT `func_800F9730`, `func_800FFA4C`; ENDING `func_80137F64`; RPG_BAT `func_8013F350`): details per function in the ticket. `func_80137F64` and `func_80138C70` show one more IDO difference: the original reloads `D_8013C360` for a second compare (`!= 0`, then `== 1`) where IDO reuses the first load.
- Not tried or skipped on sight: the TACO `RECT`-by-value callers (`func_801571E8`, `func_80158AB0`, `func_80158CDC`, `func_80158DBC`), unrolled record loops (TACO `func_80140638`, `func_8013E4FC`, RPG_BAT `func_8013E54C`, `func_8013E4D0`, `func_8013F3F0`), main `func_80046290` (shared `lui at`), and the T-hinted switches on `D_80122EB8` (BUNKASAI `func_8013B190`, `func_80146030`, BUNKAKEN `func_80140F60`: the original loads the `lw` selector into `$v1` before two stores, IDO after them, with a `u32` override and with a `u32` local alike).
## Wave 4, list 5 (T-6050): 40 of 166 functions (6492 of 41008 bytes) plus ETC `func_8014A258`
Ticket [[tickets/T-6050-wave-4-list-5]]. Files: main 80047550, 800490C0, 80049FF0 and 15 overlays (31 C files). Progress 3539 -> 3580 of 6958 functions. 49 rows added to [[data/t0018-cases]].

New patterns (matched):
- **Implicit int explains most "copy" lines.** An `or v0,rX,zero` (or `move v0,rX`) in a branch delay slot, or `move v0,zero` on an early exit, means the function is `s32 f(void)` that falls off its end (no `return`, or `return 0;` on the early path): DATE `func_801573F0`, ETC `func_80149D3C`, `func_80149DD8`, `func_80149928`, `func_8014AA74` (`case` bodies end in `break`, not `return;`), BUNKA_SD `func_80134540`, TACO `func_80134500`, DATE `func_8014A39C`, GEKO `func_8013BB4C`. Do this before anything else when a switch or a compare chain differs only by that line.
