- **Post-increment compare, boolean form.** `if (D_800E6280.unk_1104.w++ == 1)` compiles to `xori v0,v1,1; sltiu v0,v0,1; ...; beqz v0` only in an implicit-int function (`s32 f(void)`, no `return`); in a `void` function IDO emits `xori; bnez`. SHOUGATU `func_80139C10`, DATE `func_8015A114`, SHUGAKU `func_80134D8C`, `func_80134DE8`.
- **Constants of different types are not shared.** Two tests against `1` on an `s32` and on a `GsWord` get one shared `li v0,1` when both are signed; reading the word as `.u` (`unk_1104.u == 1`, `.u++ == 1`) gives the original's `li at,1` per test (DATE `func_80152C3C`, SHUGAKU `func_80134DE8`).
- **Write the globals directly, not through m2c's locals.** m2c puts a promoted global into `var_v0`; the original is `if (D == 0) { f(); D += 1; } if (D == 1) ...` and the local copy moves the register (GEKO `func_801337EC`). The same for a call result: `u8 r = func_80051A68(...)` and `(r & 0x7F)` twice, never a `temp = r & 0x7F` local (DATE `func_80139154`, `func_8013A898`, GEKO `func_801331A0`, `func_8013375C`, `func_80133250`).
- **Permuter hints that were real:** `s16 *mode = &D_80120666; switch (*mode & 1)` (ETC `func_80147414`, the candidate had score 10, not 0, yet it matched); statement pairs on one source line (TACO `func_80144D90`: `r.x = ..; r.y = ..;` on one line and `r.w = 8; r.h = 1;` on the next give the original store order, FAKE comment); GetWorkBase needs the array element twice (`ret = a[i].f8 + (a[i].f4 * 4)`), not a pointer.
- **Frame slot 4 bytes too high (sp+0x2C against 0x28).** An 8-byte local that is only written at its first word (`s32 sp28[2]`, DATE `func_80138418`) or an unused `s32 pad` declared above the real one (KANGEI `func_80134EA8`) reproduces the original, both with a `FAKE` comment. When the register local is the unused one, declare it first (ETC `func_8014AA74`: `s32 temp_v0; s32 sp28;`).
- **Bit tests with `sll 30; bgez`** are a one-bit bit-field (KANGEI `func_80134120`: `typedef struct { u32 pad0 : 1; u32 flag : 1; u32 rest : 30; }` cast over `unk_1BC[i].unk_0C`).
- **Negative scaled index into a `u8` array**: `*(s32 *)&D_80150720[-(func_80066A84() * 4)]` gives `sll; negu; lui; addu; lw lo(a2)` (ETC `func_80146BC4`); `D_80150720[-f()]` on an `s32[]` negates before the shift.
- **Struct-array fields of the table at `D_800B0A04`** (`Rec34`): `D_800B0A04[4].unk_06 += 2; D_800B0A04[4].unk_0A -= 0x14;` keeps the original order of the two `lh` (separate symbols let as1 hoist the second load above the store; EVENT `func_80107684`, `func_80107564`).
- String functions (`S`) match with UTF-8 literals as written, no island work needed in these overlays (DATE `func_8013808C`, `func_80135B4C`, `func_80133DE8`, `func_8013CADC`, KANGEI `func_801352A4`).

Blocked, by cause (rows in [[data/t0018-cases]]):
- **Struct field against separate symbols.** A `u8` field read twice (`unk_F5E` as a record index and decremented, DATE `func_801378BC`, `func_801386C8`, `func_80137B78` family) is promoted to `$v0` by IDO and kept in `$t6` by the original; scheduling of several `GameState` byte stores differs (`func_8015745C`, `func_801395B4`, KANGEI `func_80135438`). Same family as the load-hoisting notes of T-5100. (T-7010: not a view problem; the scalar view gives the same words. KANGEI `func_80135438` is two bit-field stores and DATE `func_8015745C` line-based scheduling, both matched.)
- **Variable-first compare after a branch or call** (`bne v0,v1` with the constant in `$v1`, DATE `func_8013D2C8`, `func_8013D23C`, `func_8013BDB4`, `func_8013E3C4`): V-class of T-5010, no C form.
- **Shared constants** (T-3001): runs of equal-valued stores or compares (DATE `func_8013FA6C`, `func_8013DCC0`, GYOZI `func_8014210C`, `func_8013BB0C`, TACO `func_8014488C`, RPG_BAT `func_8013F5C8`/`694`/`760` with a shared `lui a1,0x500`, `li a0,0x38` doubling as a multiplier and a call argument in GYOZI `func_80134D9C` and DATE `func_8013F544`).
- **Unrolled loops with a run-time shape** (TT `func_80132B88`, NAME_ENT `func_80133FC4` with `i` in `$v0`, ETC `func_80142AE8` with a hoisted end pointer: IDO emits `divu` remainder code for `p != &D_yyy`).
- DATE `func_80132000` matches instruction for instruction except that the original starts with a `nop`; nothing in C emits it.
- Tooling: `sync_protos.py --write/--fix` deletes the comment line above `extern u8 D_800EAFA0[];` in `include/main_api.h` every time it rewrites the file (restore it by hand); `permute.py` prints candidates with a non-zero score that the pretty-printed C already beats, so rebuild the candidate before discarding it. `queue.py --files` lists other object files of the same overlay.
- Lever: ETC `func_8014A258` (matched) is the representative of a group of 12 identical functions (`rep+11`) in other overlays; `tools/dupes.py --apply` would fill them, but they are outside this list.
## Wave 4, list 7 (T-6070): 24 functions, 5,520 bytes of the list's 127 functions (38,952 bytes)
Ticket [[tickets/T-6070-wave-4-list-7]]. The list held the leftovers of waves 2 and 3, so the yield is low (see the end). Patterns that worked:
- **Statements on one source line are scheduled as a unit.** `r.w = 0x20; r.h = 0x80;` on one line gives the original's `li w; li h; sh h; sh w` order, the same two statements on two lines give `sh w; sh h` (TT `func_80142DA8`, with `RECT r` and `func_8009C884(&r, arg0)`). The permuter found it (`do { ... } while (0)` on one line has the same effect). It is formatting, not C semantics, so the line carries a `FAKE` comment. The same `RECT` setup in TT `func_80142D40` matches this way, its 64-iteration loop is unrolled by IDO and not by the original (below).
- **`for (i = 0, p = base; ...)` allocates the registers in another order than `p = base; for (i = 0; ...)`.** TT `func_80144CA8` (`q = D_80158A6C; for (i = 0, p = D_80158AA4 + 0x64; i < (u32)(2 - q[0xC]); i++) { *p = 0; p -= 0x64; }`): the loop shape (the `beq` guard on `q[0xC] != 2`, `lbu` of `q[0xC]` inside the loop) is the for-loop with an unsigned bound; init in the header puts the counter in `$v1` and the pointer in `$a0` as in the original. TAIIKU `func_801448D4` moved from 24 to 3 differing words by the same change.
- **Implicit-int functions without a trailing `return`** (the `or v0,v1,zero` in the first delay slot of a switch chain): `s32 f(void) { switch (...) { ... break; } }`, `return;` turned into `break;` (TACO `func_80137BD8`, OPTION `func_8013AC30`). The other form: a function that ends in a call whose result the original returns without a copy: main `func_8004500C` calls `func_80044750` as the last statement of both branches and has no `return` after the stores; an explicit `var = func(); ... return var;` costs two `move`s.
- **Four `s16` arrays filled in one loop** (the `func_8004F870` setup shape again): OPTION `func_801325A0` is `typedef struct { s16 a[8]; s16 b[8]; s16 c[8]; s16 d[8]; } Arrs4x8;` and `for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) { p->a[i] = -0x78; p->b[i] = -0x58 + i * 0x14; p->c[i] = 0x60; p->d[i] = 0x14; }`; IDO unrolls it by 4 with four ramp registers exactly as the original has. Not the same as OPTION `func_80132624` (same arrays of 16 with a branch per element: IDO unrolls, the original does not).
- **`i = arg0;` copy of an argument** reproduces `move t6,a0` before the address computation (TACO `func_80137CBC`: `i = arg0; p = &D_8015F440[i * 2];` with `s32 D_8015F440[]`). `FAKE`, real source unknown.
- **Init-function twins of a matched loader** (ENDING `func_80134EF0`, `func_80135330`, `func_80135660`, `func_80135770`, `func_80135880`, `func_80135990`, `func_80135AA0`, `func_80135BB0`, 2,176 bytes; twin `func_80134DE0`): `neardupes.py --apply --check` rejects them because the new `D_8013C664..` externs are missing in the header. Write the m2c draft, add the externs to `include/ovl/ENDING.h`, and read the one `s16` source word as `*(s16 *)0x801E2404` like the twin: the symbol form `D_801E2404` costs a register (`ori t3` against `t2`). Same shape as DATE `func_8014CC40`: 17 constant stores in a row; ENDING `func_80133C10` (780 bytes) is the same with 49 separate `s32` externs and matches from the plain draft.
- **Narrow parameters in the prototype instead of casts** (TACO `func_80143730`, `func_80143814`, 448 bytes): `func_8004435C(u16 x, u16 y, u16 w, u16 h, s32 dst)` in `main_api.h` (was `()`), and the C passes `x`, `x + 0x40`, `y + 0x80` without casts. The conversion node is then `u16`, so the temp that IDO spills across the calls is reloaded with `lhu` like the original; `(u16)` casts in a K&R call spill an `int` (`lw`). Two named locals `s32 x, y` plus two spilled temps give the right 0x48 frame; four named locals give frame +8. All other callers of `func_8004435C` build unchanged, so no override was needed (`sync_protos.py --prune` not needed).
- **Frame and slot fixes by an unused local, always `FAKE`**: TACO `func_8015B2AC` needs `s32 pad[7];` declared first (frame 0x60, spills at sp+0x2C/0x30/0x34; found by trying 7, 8, 9, 10 words: 8 and 9 miss, 7 matches); TACO `func_80143574` needs a scalar first (`s32 pad;` puts the saved pointer at sp+0x28, the T-3330 idiom) and its two byte stores `p[4] = ...; p[5] = ...;` on one source line (stores 5, 4, 6 as in the original, with `(x >> 16) & 0xFF` masks). The m2c draft is the right shape for these; only `arg1->unk0` style selectors have to become `s16 *arg1` indexing (`arg1[3]`) and TACO's `TcObj50`/`TcPos` types can be used as they are.
- **Switch dispatchers need prototypes of their targets above them**: TACO `func_80137A60` calls three functions defined later in the same file; without `void f(void);` in `include/ovl/TACO.h` IDO declares them `int` and the later definition is a redeclaration error. It is an implicit-int dispatcher like `func_80137BD8` (`s32 f(void)` without `return`).
- **Near-duplicates by hand**: RPG_BAT `func_8013D290` is `func_8013D1D0` with `0x3D`; both need `D |= 0x80` / `D &= ~0x80` (`~0x80`, not `0xFF7F`: the original loads `-129` into a register) and `D_8015EDB4++; if (D_8015EDB4 >= N)`.
- **`tools/dupes.py` and `neardupes.py` report on list 7**: dupes finds only two plans, neardupes can fill 14 functions of which 8 were in my files (above); `SHOUGATU/func_8013F190`, `GYOZI/func_801418B0` and `DATE/func_80132458` are in files of other lists.

Findings that block many functions of this list:
- **GameState scalars (about 15 functions, 6 with a T-0018 row).** A `u8`/`u16`/`s32` that the original reads as a plain global (`lui v0; lbu v0,%lo(D)(v0)`) is now a member of `GameState D_800E6280`, and IDO compiles it as `lui a0; lbu a0,off(a0)` with the base in a second register. Every temp after it shifts by one, so the functions miss by register numbers only (OPTION `func_80132AB8`, `func_801387E4`, `func_8013C780`, TACO `func_80136C60`, ENDING `func_80134B18`, DATE `func_8014E38C`, main `load_palette`, `load_csr_ab`, `load_csr_tp`, KANGEI `func_80132354`, ...). The `*(u8 *)0x800E76A8` absolute form gives `lui; ori; lbu` instead (no better). Only `config/migrate_globals.txt` `keep` lines (not editable in this wave) could keep the old symbol for such a use; the 47 functions that did gain from the struct in T-5100 are the opposite case. (T-7010: wrong diagnosis. With the old scalars IDO emits the same instruction words for these functions; the misses are register allocation, see "Game-state views audit (T-7010)".)
- **Two stores of one constant through one register**: EVENT `func_80102C0C` (`li v0,8; sb v0,A; sb v0,B`), GEKO `func_80141514` (8, 0x4D, 8: the first and third share `$v0`), GYOZI `func_8013AC38` and SHOUGATU `func_8013A688` (`D = 6; f(6)`: the store takes the argument register `$a0`). `f(D = 6)` and `t = 8; D1 = t; D2 = t;` compile like the plain statements. The same group as the shared `lui $at` (main `func_8004482C`: two `sb` through one `lui at`, `InitMouse`: two `sw` of one `lui`).
- **Unrolled by IDO, not by the original**: TT `func_80142D40` (64 iterations of four `sh zero` over 0x80-byte records: `for (i = 0; i < 0x2000; i += 0x80)`, `do while`, struct array, pointer: all unrolled by 4), OPTION `func_80132624`, TAIIKU `func_80145044` (4 records, `p < &D_8011EDE0` and `p < D_8011ECD0 + 0x110` both give the register order `v0`/`v1` swapped; the 4 trailing `-=` statements on separate `D_` symbols hoist their loads as the original does).
- **`s16` sums in registers**: DATE `func_8014E010`, `func_8014DB98`, EVENT `func_80108A24` (rows in [[data/t0018-cases]]): `s16 t = D + 1; if (t >= 4) t = 0; D = t;` sign-extends into a new register; the original extends in place.
- **Aliasing reloads**: TT `func_8013F878` (the `u16` at +0x364 is reloaded in both arms of the `u8` read-modify-write at +0xF2, and `srl`/`sra` follow the `u32` temp), GYOZI `func_80135988` (the original stores `D_80145EB4` in a delay slot and again inside the next `if`; IDO drops the second), RPG_BAT `func_801495DC` (three stores of `D_8015EBB0` kept by the original, one by IDO; the stack slots match with `sp30, var_v1, sp2C, temp_v0, var_v1_2` declared in that order), TAIIKU `func_80143D24` (the original keeps `&D_8014A13C` in a register for three `lh/sh` at +0x16: it is a member of the 0xBC-byte record `D_8014A110`, not declared yet).
- **Frame**: TACO `func_80147444` (`RECT r; s32 pad;` fixes the frame and the offsets, the temps are `t1`/`t0` where the original has `t6`/`t7`; `arg1` is an `s32` the original spills and reloads).
- **Near misses left as asm** (diff in words): TAIIKU `func_801448D4` (3 words: `sll t4` against `t3` in the table index; table copy `Tbl3 t = D_8014A334` with a scalar declared before it, `for (i = 0, p = ...)`), OPTION `func_8013A550` (only the delay-slot copies of `move v0,a0`; every branch of the second half fills its delay slot from the target in IDO, none in the original), KANGEI `func_80135C78` (`bne` fills its delay slot with `lui v0` in IDO, with `lw ra` in the original), GEKO `func_80140EBC` (row in the T-0018 data), TT `func_8013F878` (above), EVENT `func_80100DD8` (`lhu` of `D_80094714` is loaded before the `lw` of `D_800EECBC` in the original; `do { } while (0)` around the statement gets it (permuter) but is noise).
- Tool notes: `rep.py`-style replacement of the `INCLUDE_ASM` line plus `migrate_globals.py --apply` is all the draft needs; m2c prints `D_800E7384`-style GameState uses and `migrate_globals` then reports "ambiguous" for words of `unk_1104`/`unk_1BC[n].unk_0C` (union `GsWord`): write `D_800E6280.unk_1104.u` by hand. A `sed -i` on `include/main_api.h` while a Docker command reads it once made the tool see no file; edit headers with a script that writes in place.
## Wave 4, list 4 (T-6040): 36 files, main exe and 20 overlays
Ticket [[tickets/T-6040-wave-4-list-4]]. The list held 185 functions (41996 bytes) and is spread thin: 36 files, mostly small switch dispatchers, table lookups and short loops. Most m2c drafts do not match as printed; what worked and what did not:
- **Dispatcher `switch (D) { case 0: f(); break; ... }` with `or v0,v1,zero` in the first delay slot is an implicit-int function** (as in the T-5010 guidance): `s32 f(void)` without `return`, `break` in every case, and the header line changes to `s32 f();` (`func_80060B24`, `pre_xmas`, `pre_syogatu_init`, `func_80061634`, ETC `func_80144A38`, `func_8014BB34`, `func_8014AE8C`). With code after the switch (`join_club`, `func_80060DF8`) the function stays `void`.
- **A switch on a `GsWord` field is fine in `$v1`**: `D_800E7384` declared `u32` (`.unk_1104.u`) matches `normal_date_move_place`, `normal_date_girl_in`, `select_girl`, `select_girl2`, `normal_date_three_select`, `normal_date_girl_out`. Pitfall of the batch tooling: if one block declares `extern s32 D_800E7384` and another `extern u32 D_800E7384`, `migrate_globals.py` maps both to the first type's member; apply and migrate block by block, or write `D_800E6280.unk_1104.u` directly.
- **A callee that the original calls with arguments its definition does not take** (`select_girl_init(0)`, `select_girl_main(0)`; `func_8007B5CC(x, y)` while the matched definition has no parameters): change the header to the K&R `()` or give the asm callee its parameter (`void select_girl_init(s32 arg0)`).
- **Narrow locals.** m2c types locals `s32`; the original used the narrow type: `func_80083628` (`u8 var_v1 = get_g_zyotai_h(..) & 0x7F`), and the permuter found `u8 n = 10` as the loop bound of EN_NICHI `func_80133924` (`for (i = 0; i != n; i++)` over `s32 *p`, `p += 12`; the bound as a literal gives `$s2`/`$s3` swapped).
- **Frame slots (T-3330 rule).** `func_8014ACB4` (declare `temp_v0` before `sp28`), `day_plus` and ETC `func_8014B9CC` (one unused word declared first, marked `FAKE`).
- **Real struct beats m2c scalars:** `D_80125Dxx` below 0x50 are fields of `Work80125D10 D_80125D10`; the batch files use the fields. The load-order failures of `func_8007B734` still persist (the original loads `D_80125D10` and `D_80125D14` before the first store, which IDO does not do for one struct).
- **Blocked, not T-0018 (no row):**
  - stores that share one `lui $at` in the original (RPG_BAT `func_8014EBA8`, `func_8014EBD0`, `func_8014F230`, `func_8014F500`, TACO `func_80143E80`): [[data/shared-at-groups]];
  - constants kept in a register and stored or passed several times (`normal_date_two_select_init`, `normal_date_three_select_init`, `normal_date_move_place_main`, `normal_date_girl_suddenout`, DATE2 `func_8013775C`: `li a0,56` is both the stride and the argument);
  - pointer loops that end at another symbol's address with a constant trip count (`hizuke_disp_switch`, `parameter_disp_switch`, EN_NICHI `func_80134784`; the same limit as main `func_800673B8`);
  - a callee defined later in the same file that the original calls without a prototype (`func_800853FC`/`func_80085368` call `sprite_brightness(s16, u8)`: the original passes `$s0` without `sll/sra`);
  - an `s16` argument that the callee sign-extends (DATE `func_80148924`, main `func_80079F00`, see "Recovered data types").
- **Register order (T-0018 rows added):** the original skips `$t9` (or starts the index chain at `$t0`) in the table lookups `func_8005B040`, `func_8005B2BC`, `func_8005B1A8` and GYOZI `func_8013B644`, `func_8013B6CC`, `func_8013B740`; swapped `$v0`/`$v1` promotions in `func_80086640`, `k_disp_inc2`, `func_8013BFE0`, BUNKA_SD `func_80135440`, EN_NICHI `func_80132ADC`. The permuter (75 to 240 s, 21 functions) found nothing that is plain C beyond the two above.
- Tooling note: `tools/docker.sh ninja <object>` also runs `CHECK HEADERS`, so a header problem shows as "BUILD FAIL with no cfe error"; a clean object build does not run `build/globals.ok`, run `migrate_globals.py --apply` and `--check` before a commit.
## K&R promotion rules (T-7000)
Ticket [[tickets/T-7000-shared-constants-lui-at-parameter-copies]]. The brief was three wave-4 blockers: (a) a constant reused from a register across stores against one `li` per store, (b) one `lui $at` shared by neighbouring stores, (c) a narrow parameter copied at entry (`andi t6,a0,0xff; or a0,t6,zero`) or kept in its home slot. Method as in T-5010: scratch scans over every function of the main exe and the 26 overlays (matched and not), IDO's ucode compared pass by pass, full rebuilds compared per function with the baseline, and a sweep of m2c drafts (1341 asm functions up to 0x140 bytes) built with the new and the old toolchain.

### Finding: the original promotes unsigned char/short to unsigned int (K&R)
A value loaded by `lbu`/`lhu` is used unsigned by the original almost everywhere (first consumer within five instructions, all 6958 functions):
| consumer of an `lbu`/`lhu` value | unsigned | signed |
|---|---|---|
| compare with a constant | `sltiu` 1459 | `slti` 3 |
| compare with a register | `sltu` 104 | `slt` 6 |
| right shift | `srl` 556 | `sra` 0 |
| division | `divu` 101 | `div` 4 |
IDO in ANSI mode gives the signed form for plain C (`u8 < 10` is `slti`, `u8 >> 2` is `sra`, `u8 % 0x30` is `div`); that is why the matched C carried `(u32)` casts and `U` suffixes. IDO's K&R mode (`cc -cckr`, cfe `-std0`) gives the unsigned form for plain C: unsigned char and unsigned short promote to unsigned int. The few signed cases fit C that converts to int first (TT `func_8013AA44`'s `div` is an `s32` index).

The K&R front end also converts a narrow parameter in place at entry when it is passed on to a callee without prototype: `andi t6,a0,0xff; move a0,t6` (pattern (c), 12 functions in the original). IDO's ANSI mode gives this only for a `u8`-prototyped callee.

One part of IDO's K&R ucode is not the original's: IDO drops the widening `CVT` of an unsigned narrow load, and uopt's operand order and register promotion depend on it. In the original a compare of a narrow global with a constant held in a register has the constant first (`lbu` 310 against 76, `lh` 40 against 8: uopt swaps when the left operand is not a plain variable), a word global has the variable first (`lw` 83 against 24), and a narrow local read from its stack slot has the variable first (55 against 0; parameters 7 against 0). So the original kept the widening for globals only. `tools/cvt_pass.py` now puts IDO's ANSI `CVT J<-L` back after a load of a narrow unsigned global (not before a store, conversion, array index, `++`/`--`, truth test, operand swap or narrow parameter, where ANSI writes none either; a switch temporary gets one), and its T-1321/T-5010 rules then work as before. Checked by comparing cfe's ANSI ucode with the widened K&R ucode over all 440 C files: of 1711 narrow global loads, 28 differ (24 compares and shifts written with an explicit unsigned operand, 4 operand swaps). Widening locals as well was tried: it fixes one matched function and breaks another, and the stack-slot statistic above says no.

Build: `-cckr` in `tools/cc.py` `IDO_CFLAGS` plus the widening step. With `-cckr` and the old pass, 29 of the 3507 C functions in the build changed; the widening brought most of them back. The rest were C written for ANSI promotion, and 14 functions were adjusted (all in the first T-7000 commit): `U` suffixes and `(u32)` casts dropped (DATE `func_8013D3B0`, `func_8013E614`, EVENT `func_8011A508`, main `func_800847B8`), an `s32` index or intermediate for a signed `div` (TT `func_8013AA44`, `func_8013AACC`, `get_h_yuukou`), `(s32)` for a signed subtraction (`func_80057640`), a `u32` copy (KANGEI `func_80134EA8`), a `u32` return (SHUGAKU `func_80135B68`), `for (i = 0, n = 10; ...)` (EN_NICHI `func_80133924`), `D_8015EB9C` declared `s32` (RPG_BAT `func_8013BEA8`), `s32 func_8009AD70();` (TACO `func_8014A8E0`), and GYOZI `func_80135D64` keeps its `FAKE` with the local copy the permuter found. Clean build 27/27 OK, no other C function changed.

### Verdicts
- **(c) Parameter copies: source (K&R), solved.** The copy-back at entry is the K&R front end's code for a narrow parameter passed on to a callee that has no prototype. Write the parameter with its narrow type and declare the callee K&R (`void f();`); a prototype on the callee gives IDO's `andi a1,a0,0xff` near the call instead. Matched: ETC `func_80132048` (with `void func_80042878();`), TACO `func_80136E70` (`void func_80139424();`), TT `func_8013C764`, main `func_8007B5EC`, `SD_DetectCDPeak`, `set_kanji_string` (its `s16` first parameter stays in its home slot and is read with `lh` after the call). Related: RPG_BAT `func_8014F1D4` calls `func_8014EF6C` with and without an argument, which needs the callee defined without prototype. Not matched: `SD_CalcCDAve` (two loop temporaries swapped), `func_80045318`, `func_80045414` (the original keeps the clamped parameter in its home slot and reloads it around the calls, IDO puts it in `$s0`), TT `func_80148C94` (the buffer sits 12 bytes lower in the frame), `func_8013230C`, `func_80147A18`. A modified parameter read back from its home slot (TT `func_80148B6C`, `func_80049FF0`) is IDO's spill choice, not K&R.
- **(a) Constants shared across stores: mostly source, partly open.** Three mechanisms:
  - Constant types: uopt keeps one register for a constant only between uses of the same type, and under K&R a constant that meets an unsigned operand is unsigned. So `u8 == 1` and `s32 == 1` load `li at,1` each, as in the original. The EVENT/DATE compare chains that needed `(u32)` casts or `1U` match as plain C: EVENT `func_8011A2C4`, `func_8011A4A4`, `func_80119600`, `func_80118764`, `func_80116360`, DATE `func_8013E5B0`, `func_8013BDB4`, TACO `func_8014EDCC`. DATE `func_8013A344` comes within 2 instructions (an `s32` global compared with a shared 3, constant first in the original).
  - Chain assignment to aggregate elements: IDO keeps the value in one register (`$v0`, `$v1`) for `a[i].x = a[j].x = k;` when the targets are array or record elements (the stored value is forwarded), and re-loads the constant for scalars and plain struct members (`A = B = k`, `S.a = S.b = k`), as for separate statements. EVENT `func_80102C0C` (`li v0,8; sb v0,A; sb v0,B` on two 0x44-byte records) matches with `D_800EAFA0[4] = D_800EAFA0[0x48] = 8;`. The RGB triples of `D_801217D0[k].unk_14..16` (`li v0,0x80`; `normal_date_girl_suddenin`, DATE `func_8013FA6C`, EVENT `func_8011B5A8` and others: 118 unmatched functions store one constant from a `$v`/`$a` register twice) have this shape; the two tried come within 2 instructions (an extra `or v0,v1,zero` copy, or the `li`/`lui` order) and stay asm.
  - Constants in ugen temporaries (`li t6,3; sh t6,A; sh t6,B`, the third store later; DATE `func_8013DCC0`, EVENT `func_80119C9C`, TT `func_80136C64`, `parameter_show_init`: 41 functions): open. IDO's ugen reuses a temporary only when the next register of its rotation still holds the constant (matched `func_800418B0`); the original reuses one out of rotation. The stores come in chain order per record (b, a, later c), so these are probably chain assignments too, with the chain value kept in an ugen temporary; no IDO form found.
- **(b) Shared `lui $at`: toolchain plus data ownership, no pass.** Re-measured over the original: `lui $at` is shared between two different addresses with the same `%hi` in 255 places and re-emitted in 16233. 231 of the shared pairs are overlay data, 24 main data, and each main group is data that only the sharing file uses (`D_800B3F60`: 8004E500 only; `D_800B3D70`: 800451D0; `D_800B6990`: 80075320; `D_800CA2A4`: 8007C030; `D_800B5920..`: shared by `dec_bg_reset` in 80057390, re-emitted by DATE). `GameState` fields, which every unit uses, are never shared. With the recovered types the C reaches neighbours through one base symbol in 221 matched functions (758 places, up from 135 functions in T-5020) where the original re-emits, so a symbol-keyed as1 rule alone is wrong. The data fit this rule: the original's as1 shared `$at` for data defined in the same source file (reached through that file's own section symbol), and every other access named its own symbol. The build cannot do this until data is defined per object ([[tickets/T-3052-per-object-data-bss-split]]); an as1 pass keyed on the object that owns the data would then be uniform. The 74 functions stay asm.

### Matched (16 functions)
ETC `func_80132048`, TACO `func_80136E70`, `func_8014EDCC`, TT `func_8013C764`, main `func_8007B5EC`, `SD_DetectCDPeak`, `set_kanji_string`, RPG_BAT `func_8014F1D4`, EVENT `func_80102C0C`, `func_8011A2C4`, `func_8011A4A4`, `func_80119600`, `func_80118764`, `func_80116360`, DATE `func_8013E5B0`, `func_8013BDB4`. In the m2c sweep only `func_8014F1D4` built with the new toolchain and not with the old one (ANSI mode refuses the call with an argument against the `(void)` definition); the sweep's other matches build with both and are blocked by the P and S flags or were untried.

## Game-state views audit (T-7010)
Ticket [[tickets/T-7010-game-state-view-audit]]; model and evidence in [[game-state]] ("Views"). Wave 4 blamed about 20 misses on `GameState` members "where the original has a separate symbol". That was a misreading: IDO compiles a constant-offset member like a scalar (same words after relocation, same registers); only as1's load-over-store hoisting and uopt's reload after an indexed store differ, and the original shows the one-object behaviour for both (0 of 407 read-modify-write pairs hoisted inside the block; counters reloaded after record stores). Check a suspected view problem by compiling the function with both views before changing declarations.

Matched (9 new, plus SHOUGATU `func_8013E0F0` moved off its alias):
- **Bit-field stores.** `lui rA; lbu rB,%lo(D)(rA); ori; sb` with rB != rA is a bit-field member set; `D |= mask` loads into rA. A local LSB-first bit-field view of the flag word matches: DATE `func_8015522C`, SHOUGATU `func_8014147C` (existing `Bits64B8`), GEKO `func_8013E1A0`, VALEN `func_80133B6C`, ETC `func_80147D74` (two bits of the byte at +0xF6), KANGEI `func_80135438` (two bits through `unk_1BC[unk_F5F]`; the index is reloaded after the first store), and outside `GameState` EVENT `func_8010242C` (`D_800B0A04[1].unk_0C.b14`) and GYOZI `func_801399C0` (`D_800F53A0.girl[9].unk_10[0]`). SHOUGATU `func_8013E0F0` (`sll 20; srl 29` sum) matches as a 3-bit field, so the `D_800E66E8` alias and its `keep` line are gone. `tools/aggregate_audit.py --bitfields` lists the 56 sites inside `GameState`; a scratch scan found 84 unmatched functions with the shape anywhere (most are BUNKAKEN/BUNKASAI functions of 0x300 bytes and more).
- **Line-based scheduling**: DATE `func_8015745C` needs `D_800E6280.unk_038 = 0; for (...)` on one line (marked `FAKE`, the T-6070 idiom).

Not matched, with the cause (none is a view problem):
- BUNKASAI `func_801354B0`, `func_801511E0` (tried; `func_8013B190`, `func_80146030` have the same shape): the bit-field part matches; as1 puts the first `lui` of the store to `D_80122EBC` or of the selector `D_80122EB8` in another place than the original (one instruction pair swapped). `func_801354B0` also needs the `u32` view of `D_80122EB8` for `$v1`. Permuter: no better candidate.
- SHOUGATU `func_8013A2F8` (tried; `func_8013AFEC` and GYOZI `func_8013DC2C` have the same shape): the bit-field part matches; `D_800E6280.unk_F5F = 5; func_800847B8(5)` with the store taking `$a0` (shared constant, T-3001).
- `load_palette` (`load_csr_ab`, `load_csr_tp` are the same shape): `s32 load_palette(s32, u8, u8, u8, u8)` with a local index gives everything except the address register (`$a0`, so `arg0` is spilled) where the original uses `$t0`; it needs a `void`->`s32` override of the `main_api.h` prototype. Left as asm.
- DATE `func_801395B4` (the `lui` of `unk_F5F` placed before `li t7,9` in the original), KANGEI `func_80132354` (`andi a0,v0` in the delay slot against `move v1,v0`), and the OPTION/TACO/ENDING/DATE/OMIMAI register cases of the T-6070 list: T-0018 family.

## Loop unrolling and scheduling (T-7020)
Ticket [[tickets/T-7020-loop-unrolling-and-scheduling]]. Wave 4 listed two blockers: (a) IDO unrolls loops the original does not, (b) IDO places loads, delay slots and shared constants differently. Verdict: both come from the C, not from the compiler. IDO 5.3 makes the same unroll decisions as the original, and its assembler schedules by the same rules. No option and no pass was adopted; `tools/cc.py` and the flags are unchanged.

### Method
- Corpus scan (scratch scripts, not committed): every function file of the main exe and the 26 overlays (SDK excluded), 1419 functions with a backward branch, each loop classified by unroll factor (offset groups against the induction step), test (`bne`, `slti`, `sltu`), remainder prologue, calls, and the order of the loads in unrolled copies. Matched functions (298 with loops) are the controls: for them IDO and the original agree by definition.
- IDO probes: scratch C compiled with the build's pipeline (`cc.py` + `cvt_pass` + `frame_pass`), with `-Wo,-unrolllimit,N` binary-searched per function to read uopt's per-copy cost, and with an `as1` shim that rewrites binasm records (`.loc`, `-O`).
- Each rule below was then tested on blocked functions; a rule counts as proven when functions match because of it.

### When a loop is unrolled (rules of IDO 5.3, which the original follows)
1. **Budget.** uopt gives each loop body a cost C and unrolls by 4 when 4C fits the limit, by 2 when 2C fits, else not; the default limit is between 264 and 341 (a 66-unit body unrolls by 4, a 171-unit body does not unroll). A body of n stores through a struct-array index costs about 3n up to 18 stores and about 9n from 19 stores on (main `func_800415B4`: 19 stores, 171, not unrolled; `func_8004164C`: 17 stores, 52, unrolled by 4 with a run-time remainder; both as in the original). OPTION `func_80132624` (16 elements, a branch per element) is unrolled by 2 by the original and by IDO alike; T-5020 read it as not unrolled.
2. **Trip count.** Constant counts: 2 or 3 trips stay a rolled loop; 4 to 7 trips are unrolled completely (straight-line code, no branch); 8 and more become an unrolled-by-4 loop with the N mod 4 left-over copies first. Count the trips from the end address (ETC `func_80140DC8` is 83 trips: 3 copies, then 20 rounds of 4). TT `func_80142D40` is a 256-trip loop over 0x20-byte steps (`for (i = 0; i < 0x2000; i += 0x20)`), not a 64-trip loop with 4 stores; it is unrolled by both compilers.
3. **Counter assigned again later: rolled.** A counted loop stays rolled (one copy, `slti`/`bne` on the counter) when its counter variable is assigned again after the loop: the same `i` in a later loop, `i = f();`, or `i = 1; return i;` (TT `func_80132B88`: first of two loops over `i`). The init has to be right before the loop; `i = 0;` before an enclosing `if` does not count. Evidence: of the 70 rolled `slti` loops without a sign extension in unmatched functions, 58 are followed by another loop in the same function, against 105 of 267 unrolled-by-4 loops.
4. **Loops without a conditional back edge are never unrolled** (`for (;;) { ...; if (++i == n) break; }`, a `goto` loop). Narrow counters (`s8`, `s16`, `u8`) are never unrolled either; they show as `sll/sra` or `andi` on the counter.
5. **Nested loops.** An inner constant loop of 4 is unrolled completely and the outer loop stays rolled with the end address in a register (main `func_800673B8`: `for (i = 0; i < 32; i++) {` / `for (j = 0; j < 4; j++) D_800E6280.unk_163C[i * 4 + j] = 0;` / `}`). T-5020 found no C for this "hoisted end pointer with a constant trip count".
6. **Pointer loops.** `p < end` is never unrolled (`sltu`). `p != start + N` (same symbol) is not unrolled. `p != &OTHER` gets a run-time remainder (`subu; andi 0x3F`), which no original function has: the original's pointer-compare loops are index loops that uopt strength-reduced. Write the index loop (`D_8011ECD0[0x443 + i * 0x44]`, `D_8013984C + i * 0x30`).

### Scheduling (rules of IDO 5.3's as1, which the original follows)
1. **Source lines.** as1's scheduler uses the `.loc` line records. Proof that the original's assembler did the same: a full build with every `.loc` removed from the binasm changes 906 of the matched functions. Consequence for unrolled loops: with the body on the line of its `for`, IDO loads copy 0 first (`lbu 0x443; lbu 0x487; ...`); with the body on its own line it loads copy 0 last. In the original, 73 straight-line unrolled loops load copy 0 first and 34 last; matched: hizuke_disp_switch, parameter_disp_switch, OPTION `func_80134210`, `func_801385F0`, `func_80137FA0`, TACO `func_80133410` with one-line loops.
2. **Fully unrolled loops explain "load hoisting" and `lui` placement.** A run of read-modify-writes on fields one record apart, each `lh` after the previous `sh`, with the next `lui` above the previous store, is a 4-7 trip loop that IDO unrolls completely (TAIIKU `func_80140338`: `for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) *(s16 *)&D_8011ECD0[0x1AA8 + i * 0x44] -= 4 - i;`; `func_8014038C`, `func_801450C0`: `+= t.v[i]` from a local copy of a 4-entry table; `func_80148820`, `func_80148678`, `func_80145044`, `func_801487B4`). Written as separate statements on separate symbols, IDO's as1 moves the loads up (T-6010, T-6070).
3. **One base symbol keeps the loads in place.** as1 moves a load above a store only when the two have different symbols; neighbours read after a store go through one base symbol (`*(s16 *)&D_8011ECD0[0x1998]`, `D_801206D8[0x1037]`), as CODING_STANDARDS 8 says for aggregates.
4. **Delay slots and init order.** In TT `func_80132C24` the original fills the `bne` delay slot with the counter init (`move v1,zero`); IDO does that when the counter is initialised before the pointer (`i = 0; q = D_80158AA8; for (; ...)`), the order that also gives the original's pointer/counter registers (TT `func_80146BE0`, `func_80146C14`).
5. **Shared constants by type.** `p[0x1D] == 0x80U` keeps the compare's `li at,128` apart from the loop bound's `li a1,128`; `== 0x80` shares one register (T-6050 note on constants of different types).

### Options and passes tested (none adopted)
- `-Wo,-unrolllimit,N`, `-Wo,-nomultibbunroll`: the T-5020 rebuilds stand (changes to matched functions below N = 200); the blocked loops did not need them once the C was right.
- as1 `-O0/-O1/-O3`: the binasm `.option O 2` record overrides them ("Binasm file dictates -O: 2"); with the record rewritten nothing changes the load order.
- Dropping or flattening `.loc` records before as1: fixes the copy-0 order of one-line-less loops but changes 906 matched functions (rule 1 of Scheduling), so the original was line-sensitive.
- uopt `-zdbug` is not usable in the recompiled uopt (T-3100); the cost model above was read with `-unrolllimit` instead.

### Matched (T-7020)
28 functions: TT `func_80132B88`, `func_80132C24`, `func_801462AC`, `func_8014687C`, `func_80146BE0`, `func_80146C14`; main hizuke_disp_switch, parameter_disp_switch, k_disp_start, k_disp_goto_line_end, get_now_k, `func_8006B014`, `func_80053564`, `func_800673B8`; OPTION `func_80134210`, `func_801385F0`, `func_80137FA0`; TACO `func_80133410`; ETC `func_80140DC8`, `func_80140E80`; EN_NICHI `func_80134784`; TAIIKU `func_80145044`, `func_80148820`, `func_80148678`, `func_80140338`, `func_8014038C`, `func_801487B4`, `func_801450C0`. One-line loop bodies carry a `FAKE` comment, as the one-line statements of T-6070 do (73 of 107 original unrolled loops load copy 0 first, so the original source probably had short loops on one line). Later, 25 more from the helper lists: main search_tpage_multi, `func_80052E60`, `func_80060EA0`, `func_8006AEC4`, `func_80071110`; DATE `func_801517C8`; EN_NICHI `func_801340CC`; 14 EVENT functions through `Rec34` fields of `D_800B0A04`; TAIIKU `func_8013732C`, `func_80144B40`; TT `func_8013AC0C`. Total 53, progress 3721 -> 3774.
