## Tooling fixes, wave 4 (T-7030)
Ticket [[tickets/T-7030-tooling-wave-4-bug-fixes]]. Results first: `dupes.py --apply --check` applied 163 of 174 planned copies (before: 0 of 5 planned, 201 unmatched members skipped without a way to see why), `neardupes.py` 25 of 25 (before: 0 of 15); 188 functions in C, progress 3721 -> 3909 of 6958, clean build 27 of 27 sha1 OK, `headers OK`, `globals OK`. Left: 38 unmatched dupes members and 18 near-dupes members, each with a reason in `-v` / `--report`.
- **Why the 20 failed** (`dupes.py -v` now prints the reason of every skip; the 5 plans and 15 near plans built nothing for these reasons):
  - *Override views not copied* (DATE `func_8014F370` family, GEKO `func_8015A3B0`: 3 of the 5 dupes plans, 9 of the 15 near plans): the source file declares the selector `u32` through `MAIN_API_OVERRIDE_D_80122CD0`; the copy compiled the `s32` of `main_api.h` and the switch selector sat in `$v0` instead of `$v1`. Now copied (define + declaration) into the target file.
  - *Address literals* (4 of the 15 near plans: DATE `func_80132458`, GYOZI `func_801418B0`, SHOUGATU `func_8013F190`, `func_80134890`; pointer-table initialisers): the C writes `*(s16 *)0x801F0D14` for `%lo(D_801F0D14)`; the copy kept the source's address. Now mapped through the relocation sequence.
  - *Header clashes of the plan itself*: `D_801206B0` appended to `main_api.h` by hand clashed with EVENT's own `s32` at that address (EVENT overlays the main bss; `sync_protos.py --fix` writes the `MAIN_API_OVERRIDE_` guard), and a source `void` against the target's `s32` prototype (`func_801323D4`) or the other way round (`uwasa0`: callers said `void`, the twin returns a value).
  - *Game-state names* (about 75 of the 196 dupes skips; TEL `func_801361CC` and `func_801362B4`, the twins of `func_801360E4`, among them): `D_800E6280.unk_110D` in the C, `D_800E738D` in the asm relocation. Now resolved through the address.
  - *Constants that merge* (EVENT `func_8011A2C4`, `func_8011A4A4`): sources 2 and 3 became 1 and 1; IDO loads `li v0,1` once and shares it, the original has two `li at,1`. Now skipped with that reason.
  - Not a cause, checked: rodata islands (the J and S flags: jump-table functions are excluded from fingerprints on purpose) and functions moved into per-object files (the C file is found through the asm folder of the splat config).
  - Also found while building the 163: a copy that calls a function the target file defines further down needs a prototype in the header (an implicit `int f()` clashes with the later `void f(void)`: cfe "redeclaration"); a call statement such as `    f();` must not be mistaken for a prototype when the source `.c` is searched; a typedef of the target `.c` (`FnTbl20`) cannot be used in a header declaration; a source callee used as `f(...) == 1` against a `void` prototype of the target does not compile (skipped).
  - Still skipped, by design: views of an overlay-private global that differ in type (`s16`/`u16`, `s32`/`u8 *`, scalar/array: 12 of the 38), an element of the `GameState` struct array against a plain global (IDO loads a struct-array element and a scalar in another order, [[data-types]]), constant-index elements of an array (`D_80120650[7]`: the relocation names `D_80120657`), and struct types of another overlay's header (`GyoziRec44`, `TaiikuBig`).
- **`sync_protos.py --write/--fix` deleted comments.** `render_api` rebuilt the globals and functions sections from the declarations only. It now keeps every comment, `#if` block and type definition that is not a declaration of a symbol, and a comment stays with the declaration below it when the address order changes (test `test_write_keeps_comments_and_other_lines_it_does_not_own`). The `types_block` of T-5100 (between the includes and `/* ---- globals ---- */`) is kept as before. `fix_header` still drops a one-line label comment above a declaration it moves to `main_api.h` (only the labels listed in its regex; a longer remark is kept and logged).
- **`funcdiff.py` and `expected/`.** The reference object is now built from the original `.s` files with `tools/cc.py` (all functions of the file as `INCLUDE_ASM`, whatever the C looks like) and refreshed when one of them changes. The lesson at "Process lesson: `expected/` objects must be copied from a build made before ANY edit" no longer applies.
- **`permute.py`.** (a) Options work before or after the subcommand and the function. (b) `--time` is a wall-clock limit for the whole process group (SIGINT, SIGKILL after ten seconds; the old code killed only the parent and left its workers) with a progress line every 10 s. The permuter itself needs 1 to 10 s for the small functions tried here. (c) A score of 0 is verified with ninja and `funcdiff --resolve`. Cause of "score 0, ninja differs" (main `func_8004111C`, reproduced): the permuter's output keeps four statements on one line (`do { rect.x = 0; rect.y = 0; rect.w = 640; rect.h = 480; } while (0);`, the `perm_sameline` pass); IDO numbers the stores of one source line together and builds the original's order (`sh h; sh w; sh x; sh y`). The same four statements on lines of their own build the other order (verified: one line `MATCH`, four lines `DIFF`). Pasting a candidate reformatted, or with the `do { } while (0)` removed and the statements split, changes the bytes; the verification pastes it verbatim and now notes lines with several statements. TT `func_8013C764` has no C yet and could not be reproduced; EVENT `func_8011A508` was the extern-type edit below. (d) The passes `perm_randomize_external_type` and `perm_randomize_function_type` (types of externs, return and parameter types of the function and its callees) have weight 0 in `settings.toml` and a candidate whose declarations differ from `base.c` is reported with the changed tokens. They do find real facts (EVENT `func_8011A508`: `D_800EECBC` as `unsigned long` was the fix, in practice a `MAIN_API_OVERRIDE_` view); run with `--allow-decl-edits` when the near miss looks like a type problem, and apply such a type change to the declaration yourself.
- **`m2c.py`.** Wrong argument counts: the prototypes of `main_api.h` and the overlay header were already in the context; the wrong counts are callees declared `()` or not at all, where m2c takes every argument register that holds a value (stale ones too). The wrapper now counts the parameters in the callee's own asm (295 of 313 prototyped main-exe functions agree) and lists the counts on stderr. Reverse post-increment: m2c stores first and compares the variable; the wrapper rewrites to `D++` where the asm compares the old register (78 asm functions match the shape; 6 of the 8 matched ones use `++`). Unfixable here: SDK/GTE callees without readable asm, indirect calls, and whether IDO then builds the original's boolean form. Details: [[decompile-workflow]], "m2c drafts".


## Wave 5, list 5 (T-8050): 24 functions matched, 7 copied by dupes.py
List: 160 functions in 37 files, nearly all of them earlier failures (115 of them already named in this file or in [[data/t0018-cases]]). The 24 matches: BUNKA_SD `func_80135440`; ETC `func_801395E8`, `func_8013AEE4`, `func_8013B078`, `func_80137A14`, `func_80137C7C`, `func_801380AC`, `func_8013872C`; EVENT `func_800FCDD8`, `func_8010B490`, `func_801073FC`, `func_80106F90`; NAME_ENT `func_8013B5F0`, `func_8013C6FC`, `func_8013C834`, `func_8013D62C`, `func_8013D874`, `func_8013DC84`, `func_8013E71C`, `func_8013FF98`, `func_80140E18`; SHUGAKU `func_80133758`; TT `func_80133B70`, `func_8013F650`. `dupes.py` filled seven GYOZI twins in `8013C3B0.c` (`func_8013C5E8`, `func_8013C6EC` and five more). Five near misses stay behind `#ifdef NON_MATCHING` (KANGEI `func_80138D40`, NAME_ENT `func_801454CC`, `func_80140D6C`, TT `func_80147074`, GYOZI `func_8013E724`); rows in [[data/t0018-cases]].
- **What worked, by shape.**
  - *Dialog and screen-setup functions with literals* (NAME_ENT, 6): the m2c draft with the literals as UTF-8 and the strings of `D_800B3C70`/`D_800B3C88` passed as `u8` arrays matched at once; `func_8004E788` takes an `s32` and the call sites hand it a `char *`, as the other overlays do. The string used by `func_8013D62C` needs a 0x100-byte local and a leading unused `s32` (FAKE, T-3330) to put the buffer at sp+0x34 in a 0x138 frame.
  - *ETC screen setup* (7 matches, sibling style of `func_801369D4`): `load_palette`, the `D_80120650[off]` byte view for the sprite table, `*(s16 *)&D_80120650[0x2A] = -0x65`, and the 0x44-byte record copy written as a struct assignment. The record type must have word alignment (`s32 w[0x11]`): with `u8 b[0x44]` IDO copies with `lwl/lwr` pairs (12 words wrong).
  - *Function-pointer tables*: `s32 idx;` declared first and used (`idx = D_800B1AF6; tbl.f[idx](0x80)`) gave EVENT `func_8010B490` (table at sp+0x2C, frame 0xD8); without it the table sits at sp+0x28 and the frame is 8 bytes smaller. EVENT `func_800FCDD8` (table at sp+0x30, no scalar) needed a permuter find: `if (r == (D_800B1AE4 * 0))` moves the load of `D_800B1AE4` into $v1 (FAKE, kept with its comment).
  - *Jump-table switches in a per-object island* (EVENT `func_80106F90`): plain `switch` with `return` in every case, nothing else. funcdiff shows the table relocations only; the unit sha1 decides.
  - *Temp numbering follows the order of the source expression.* TT `func_8013F650`: `*(s32 *)(p + 0x20) = *(s32 *)(p + 0x3E4) + (call(...) << 9)` gives the original numbering, the other operand order is one temp off. NAME_ENT `func_8013B5F0`: the division statement first, then the sum, and `D_8014D0E4 += D_8014D0EC` without a local (a local sum lands in $v0).
  - *Bit-fields of a packed date word* (NAME_ENT `func_8013E71C`): `sll 23; srl 27` is a bit-field read, a `{day:4; month:5; week:2}` view cast on the word.
  - *Rec34 neighbours* (EVENT `func_801073FC`): `D_800B0A04[4].unk_02 += 2` etc. match; the scalar `D_800B094E` next to them has its load hoisted by IDO, the original keeps it in order: write it as `*(s16 *)((u8 *)D_800B0A04 - 0xB6) += 0xA` (FAKE, same base symbol as the neighbouring stores).
  - *TT `func_80133B70`* (T-6050 recorded it as register order): the T-7020 form `i = 0; p = X; for (; i < 0x80; i++) { *p = 0; p += 0x68; }` gives pointer $v1, counter $v0, bound $a0.
- **Same shape, still open.**
  - `or v0,v1,zero` in the delay slot of a compare-chain switch (OLH `func_80135DD4`, RPG_BAT `func_8013F4F0`, `func_80142000`, NAME_ENT `func_8013D6C0`): the code is otherwise identical; a `u8` local selector does not produce the copy.
  - `D_800E7313` family in NAME_ENT (`func_8013D4CC`, `func_8013DAC4`, `func_8013E430`, `func_8013F454`, `func_8013FB8C`, `func_8013CEC8`): all listed with the T-0018 reason already.
  - Constant folded by IDO but not by the original: RPG_BAT `func_80132B34` (`0x84` and `0x84 + 0x30` come from one register in the original).
  - DATE `func_8014AD9C` was written (PsyQ MATRIX layout, rotation calls) but needs `D_801227A0` as `u8[]`; TAIIKU keeps it as `TaiikuBig[]`, so it stays asm until that symbol has one home. TT `func_801349B0` needs `func_8014D260` to return a value (declared `void`).
  - Tried and reverted, near but not matching: DATE `func_80148924` (the original sign-extends the `s16` argument once; IDO drops it), TACO `func_801357B0` (the original has a dead `move v0,zero` in the delay slot), `func_80143E80`/`func_80143F40`, main `func_8006C988` (the pointer walk with a counter differs in register use), `func_8004E500` (the RECT sits 4 bytes lower in the original; four `s16` locals or a `s16[4]` do not move it), NAME_ENT `func_80145EA4` (permuter best 1040), TT `func_80134D0C` (permuter best 915), `func_80134ADC`, `func_80133AD0`, `func_80134874`.
- **Tool notes.** `cycle.sh`-style batches (migrate, `sync_protos --write/--fix`, then one funcdiff call with several `UNIT:func` names) cost one build per round; with eight agents on the Docker host a full ninja took 10 to 25 minutes, so write many functions per round. `permute.py all` found the one FAKE above and confirmed ten dead ends (150 s each). `dupes.py --apply` also edits files owned by other lists (GYOZI, SHOUGATU twins); revert the ones outside your list.
## Wave 5, list 1 (T-8010)
Ticket [[tickets/T-8010-wave-5-list-1]]. 20 functions matched (see the ticket for the list). Patterns that worked and the blockers of the rest:
- **Local function-pointer table (T-3330), sizes seen:** copy loop to 0x24 (9), 0x94 (37), 0xA0 (40), 0xB0 (44), 0xDC (55), 0xE0 (56), 0xF4 (61); the table type is a file-local `FnTblN`. Frame check: if the built table sits 4 bytes too high, declare `s32 idx;` (unused, FAKE comment) before it; if 8 too high (frame 8 short), the same single `s32` fixes both (KANGEI `func_80134DB0`, DATE `func_801563A8`, `func_80156494`, `func_801571D4`, ENDING `func_80135DD0`, EVENT `func_801087F8`, `func_80109EE8`). Two tables (TAIIKU `func_80138950`: `TaiikuTbl11 a, b; a = X; b = Y; f(a.v[idx], b.v[idx], idx)`) need no pad. The index is read from the global at each use when the original does (no `idx` variable).
- **Stage-banner dispatchers (TACO `func_801480B0`, `func_80138450`)** are plain: `(u32)GameState.unk_1100 < 0x60 && (unk_10F8 & 0x10)` banner call with a string literal cast `(s32)"..."`, optional `unk_1104.w += 1`, `switch (unk_110A)`, then three common calls. Callees defined further down the file need prototypes in the overlay header.
- **`s32` return type gives the dead `or v0,v1,zero`** of a switch on an unsigned global (OLH `func_801352A0`: the function falls off its end, IDO keeps the selector copy in `$v0`). It is the rule of "Selector register rule (T-5010)" seen from the other side: write the dispatcher `s32 f(void)` without a `return`, update the overlay header.
- **`case 0xFF: default:` share the default block** when the original's compare chain has an explicit 0xFF compare that jumps to the default call (OPTION `func_8013C780`).
- **Switch prologue `or t7,a0,zero; andi a0,t7,0xff`** (main `func_8007A43C`) is `void f(u8 arg0) { arg0 &= 0xFF; switch (arg0) ... f(arg0) }`; found by the permuter, plain `u8 arg0` or `s32 t = arg0 & 0xFF` do not give the copy. A jump-table function in an `island yes` object builds and the sha1 decides (funcdiff shows only the table relocations).
- **Packet-link bit-field insert** (TT `func_80141A1C`, `func_8013D2E0`, `func_8013D3C0`, `func_8013D150`): `struct { u32 addr : 24; u32 len : 8; u8 r0, g0, b0, code; ... }` and `p->addr = ot->addr; ot->addr = (u32)p;` produce the original's 14-instruction xor/shift sequence exactly. What differs is only register assignment (original skips `t0`; `v0/v1/a1` for the locals), so none was kept.
- **Redundant store kept by the original** (GYOZI `func_80135988`): `D = v; if (c) { D = v; ... }` loses the second store in IDO; `D = v ^ 0;` (permuter) keeps it (FAKE).
- **Two statements on one source line are scheduled as a unit** again (TT `func_80133058`, main `func_80059308`: RECT field stores `rect.w = ..; rect.h = ..;`). FAKE comments in the source.
- **abs in the original** (`or v1,v0,zero; bgez; b; negu`) is `if (d < 0) a = -d; else a = d;`, not `a = d; if (d < 0) a = -d;` (TT `func_80133288`). `||` conditions with a shared call body compile like `goto call` blocks.
- **Redundant recomputation**: the permuter found that TAIIKU `func_80140738` needs the second `*(u8 **)((u8 *)D_80122640 + arg1 * 0x10 + 4)` written out instead of the pointer variable (the original reloads the spilled `p` from the stack once and recomputes for the stores). `p = ...` plus later `p[1] = ...` gives different scheduling.
- **Compare-chain classification (TAIIKU `func_8013AF74`)**: a series of `if (A < v && B ...) return n;` with the same `v` re-compared in four consecutive ifs compiles exactly like the original when written with `&&` and one `s32 v = ...;`.

### Not matched, new patterns (rows in [[data/t0018-cases]] where they are register-order cases)
- **GameState members read as scalars (known gap, T-7010):** VALEN `func_80133670`, main `func_80042960` (jump table, case list 0x11 0x13-0x15 0x20-0x22 0x35-0x37 0x3A 0x43 0x45 0x50-0x63 0x70-0x76 0x80-0x82 0x90 0x91 0xC2-0xC4), BUNKASAI `func_80146030`, DATE `func_801572E4`/`func_80155E40`, ETC `func_8014AF28`, TACO `func_8013546C`: the C is right, the base or selector register differs because the original reads `D_800E71DF` & co. as separate symbols.
- **Constant hoisting before a branch (original loads `li v0,0x140; li v1,0xF0` once above the `if`, IDO propagates them into both arms):** main `strSetDefDecEnv`, `func_800590CC`/`func_800591D8` (a0/a1 = 0x140/0xF0).
- **Original spills `arg0` and a base temp with a shared slot** (main `func_80058F9C`, sibling of TAIIKU `func_80140738`): local-variable forms give separate slots.
- **`multu` with the element size kept in `$a0`** because it is also a call argument (GYOZI `func_80136124`: `girl[idx]` and `func_8008FB00(0x38, idx)`).
- **Loads hoisted over stores** (main `func_8007B734`, `func_8007A868`: the RMW loads of `D_80125D10.unk_00`/`D_80125D14` come first, the zero stores to `D_80125D18..` last): separate `s32` externs for the later fields and temporaries do not hoist.
- **Alias between a scalar and a record table** (EVENT `func_801035BC`, DATE `func_80155E40`): the original re-reads `D_800EAFA7`/`D_80120657` inside a store loop to the 0x44-byte record table; IDO hoists it. Reading the byte as `D_800E9620[0x1A87]` stops the hoist but changes the relocation symbol.
- **Frame with three unused words above the out-parameters** (TAIIKU `func_8013C4E4`: `sp34..sp40` out words, frame 0x50): any pad gives `sp40` at 0x44.
- **`u16` argument kept in `a0` across the compare chain** of a dispatcher loop (main `func_80079B34`): IDO copies it to `a1`.
- **Temporaries one register later** (main `func_8007A354`, `func_8007B144`, EVENT `func_80108A24`): the T-0018 family of wave 4.
## Wave 5, list 6 (T-8060)
Ticket [[tickets/T-8060-wave-5-list-6]]. 29 functions, 5980 bytes, in TT, GEKO, TACO, EVENT, SHOUGATU, SHUGAKU, KANGEI, TEL, BUNKA_SD, DATE, EN_NICHI, RPG_BAT, TAIIKU. New patterns:
- **Struct-by-value calls (TACO `func_8015ACCC(a, b, TcPos, TcPos, TcPos, c, d)`).** IDO copies each 8-byte `TcPos` argument with `lwl/lwr/swl/swr` into the outgoing area, so declare the callee with the struct parameters and pass the globals/locals directly (`func_8015ACCC(0xF, 0x16, D_801604D4, pos, D_801604E4, 4, 0xD0)`). The original frame has up to three `TcPos`-sized slots more than the visible locals; unused `TcPos` locals reproduce them (`FAKE`, TACO `func_80158AB0`, `func_80158CDC`, `func_80158DBC`, `func_80156F50`): pad above and below the used local, in declaration order (the first declared sits at the top). The callee returns a value that some callers read, so it is declared `s32`.
- **Frame slots of local function-pointer tables with a `printf` before the call (EVENT).** `func_800F84E0` and `func_800F86C4` are the T-3330 idiom with `func_80078950("%d\n", D_800B1AF6)` between the table copy and the call; the copy size is the table size rounded up to the 12-byte copy loop plus the two tail words (`0xA4` and `0x8C` bytes, not the nominal `0x9C`/`0x88`). `idx = D_800B1AF6;` after the print.
- **`(u8)func_80051A68(x) & 0x7F`.** The FAKE cast of T-2020 (extra temp the original has) also works in GEKO and SHOUGATU/DATE (`func_80136F20`, `func_80137040`, `func_801345C8`, `func_80134648`, `func_80149AC0`).
- **A global read first as an argument is loaded straight into `$a0`.** EN_NICHI `func_8013556C`: writing `D_80139224 += 3; if (D_80139224 >= 0x1000) D_80139224 = 0;` and then `func_800A0070(D_80139224)` gives the original's `lw a0` / `addiu a0,a0,3`; a local `v = D + 3` puts the value in `$v0` and moves it.
- **`D_800EAFA0[4] = D_800EAFA0[0x48] = 8;` with three read-modify-writes between the two stores (EVENT `func_800F8188`).** The chain keeps `li v0,8` live across them; as two separate statements IDO loads 8 twice.
- **`0x14 == (D_801206F0 ^ 0)` (BUNKA_SD `func_8013261C`).** The only form (decomp-permuter) that keeps the shared constant as the left operand of the `bne`; `FAKE`.
- **A bitfield test needs a `u32` bit-field struct (GEKO `func_80136E7C`).** `((GekoFlagBits *)&D_800E6280.unk_1BC[i].unk_0C.w)->f` gives `sll 28; bgez`; `& 8` gives `andi`.
- **Local tables again (GEKO `func_8013A180`, 23 entries; EVENT `func_800F86C4`, 35 entries).** Both need the T-3330 `idx` declared before the table (the table then sits 4 bytes lower); sizes come from the copy loop (`bne` against `src + 0x54`/`0x84` plus the tail words), not from the nominal struct size.
- **Arrays of 16.16 positions (TT `func_8013C048`).** The integer halves are read as `s16` at `+0x22/+0x26` through a second struct view; the statement order `+=` 28, 2C, then 20, 24, then the halfword copies 14 and 16 (found with a brute-force over the 24 orders) gives the original schedule.
- **Unmatched, T-0018-like, rows in [[data/t0018-cases]]:** a global loaded into `$v1` (EVENT `func_800FD588`, SHUGAKU `func_801322F0`), a local in `$a0`/`$a1`/`$a3` (SHUGAKU `func_80132BCC`, 800420D0 `func_800422C8`, RPG_BAT `func_801426B0`, `func_80142544`), a constant kept in `$a0`/`$a1` (SHOUGATU `func_8013AB5C`, GYOZI `func_8013D764`, TT `func_8013A040`, GEKO `func_801374A8`, SHOUGATU `func_80134730`), and a pointer parameter copied to `$a2` (TT `func_8013BF9C`).
- DATE2 func_80136A90: `(rand() % 2) != 0` is folded to `rand() & 1` by this IDO; the original keeps the signed remainder (`bgez; andi 1; beqz; addiu -2; beqz`). Local variable and plain forms all fold. Unmatched.
- ETC func_8013F980: pointer var initialised with the constant 0x801FA808 is constant-folded (`lw a1,-22520(t6)`) while the original keeps it in $s2 (`ori s2`, `lw a1,0(s2)`, `addiu s2,4`); loop shape otherwise reproduced with a pointer do-while. Unmatched.
- EN_NICHI func_80132834: twelve `-1` stores to GameState unk_1228[5..10] and [37..42]: original is 2 direct copies then 8 stores through one base (`li a0,2; sll; addu v0`), a 6-trip loop with 4-unroll leftover; a plain `for (i = 0; i < 6; i++)` is unrolled differently (base+8 form). Unmatched.
## Wave 5, list 7 (T-8070): 37 files, main exe and 14 overlays
Ticket [[tickets/T-8070-wave-5-list-7]]. 34 functions matched (about 6860 bytes) out of roughly 90 tried; the rest of the list is the register-order family (rows in [[data/t0018-cases]]). What worked, in the order I would try it:
- **A local copy of the selector fixes compare operand order.** Original `bne v0,v1` / `bne v1,v0` mismatches with a constant first came from IDO canonicalising `D == k` on a global; `u8 m; m = D_800CA2FC; if (m == 0) .. if (m == 1)` and `u8 g = D_800E6280.unk_F5F;` before the first use give the original order (DATE `func_8013D2C8`, `func_8013D23C`, `func_801395B4`). It did not help when the selector is an `s32` global or a `u8` read twice in a 4-way chain (DATE `func_8013A344`, `func_8013E3C4`).
- **`sizeof(Rec38)` for a call argument that equals the multiplier of a `Rec38` index** (`func_80084D3C(sizeof(Rec38))`, SHOUGATU `func_80142784`, DATE `func_8013F544`): IDO then shares the `li` between the `multu` constant and the argument as the original does; a literal `0x38` gives a second `li` in another register.
- **`x >= 1` for an unsigned `!= 0` that the original computes with `sltiu; xori 1`** (GYOZI `func_80140330`: `(D_800F594F >= 1) * 3 + 4`). `!= 0` gives `sltu t,zero,x`.
- **A chain assignment as call argument** (`func_800847B8(D.unk_F5F = tbl[i].unk_01)`, OMIMAI `func_801337EC`) puts the value in `$a0` straight from the load; a local variable puts it in a temp and copies.
- **`s32 f(void)` with `if (D.field++ < 0x80) return 0;` and no trailing return** reproduces `sltiu; addiu; andi; ... or v0,zero` (DATE `func_8013B910`); `D++ < k` compares the old value, `++D` the new one.
- **Implicit-int switch handlers with Japanese help text** (OLH `func_80134824` and five siblings): `s32 f(void) { switch (D_800E6280.unk_110D) { case 0: ...; break; ...} }` with string literals written as UTF-8 (`カーソル`, not the `\x83\\` escape m2c prints); the headers line changes to `s32 f(void);`.
- **Switch selector in `$v1` for a word global** (BUNKAKEN `func_8013BFE0`): `MAIN_API_OVERRIDE_D_80122EB8` and `extern u32 D_80122EB8;` in the C file (`T` flag of the queue). The same trick failed where the selector is `s16`/`u16` (SHOUGATU `func_80137DE4`) or is combined with a signed constant (RPG_BAT `func_80150CD8`).
- **Bit test through a throw-away bit-field struct** (`sll 29; bltz`): `((BunkakenFlagBits *)&D_800E6280.unk_1BC[1].unk_0C)->flag` with `u32 pad0 : 2; u32 flag : 1; u32 rest : 29;` in the C file, as KANGEI `func_80134120` does for bit 1. For a call result use `(f(x) << 9) < 0` (TT `func_8014BB30`, otherwise blocked).
- **Unused first local for an unused frame slot** (`s32 pad;` before `u8 sp2B;` or before a `RECT`): the original frame has one more word above the locals (GEKO `func_801346A0`, DATE `func_8013A464`, and the earlier DATE `func_8013E6A4` shape). Marked `FAKE`; real source unknown.
- **`RECT` with `w, h` stored before `x, y`** (main `func_8004111C`): `rect.w = 640; rect.h = 480;` on one source line, then `x`, `y` on their own lines. as1 schedules per source line (`FAKE` comment).
- **8-byte records at GameState+0x758** (`D_800E69D8 + D_800E71DE * 8`, DATE `func_801378BC` family, five functions): `u8 i = unk_F5E; if (((u8 *)&D_800E6280)[0x758 + i * 8] == 1) unk_F5E = i - 1;`. The local copy of `i` is needed (`D -= 1` directly keeps the byte in `$v0`). The aggregate is not declared yet, hence the `FAKE` comments.
- **`s32` handlers whose only `or v0,zero` is on the "nothing pressed" path** (main `normal_date_two_select_main`, `normal_date_three_select_main`, TAIIKU `func_801419F8`): `if (a) { x = 0; } else if (b) { x = 1; } else { return 0; } common();` with the shared tail after the chain instead of m2c's `goto`; the header line becomes `s32 f(void);`. The `Rec` byte flags at `GameState+0xF96 + 0x10*k` are `unk_F90[2k+..].unk_06`.
Not buildable or not matched, with the reason:
- DATE `func_80132000` (and ENDING `func_80132000`): starts with a one-nop pad (`nop`, then `alabel func_80132004`); the 38-pointer initialiser itself compiles, the nop does not.
- Constants the original loads once and stores several times in straight-line code, or three `s16[2]` argument arrays with shared `li` (DATE `func_8013DCC0`, `func_8013FA6C`, `func_80137FAC`; main `normal_date_two_select_init`, `normal_date_three_select_init`, `normal_date_girl_in_init`, `normal_date_girl_suddenin`; TAIIKU `func_80141964`): same as T-3001, copy propagation is off so no C form shares the register. TAIIKU `func_80143AC8` (4 records of 4 increments) needs one `lui $at` shared across symbols (T-3052).
- `u8 * 3` as `li 3; multu` (TACO `func_80136C60`): IDO emits shift and subtract.
- Phantom registers (constants in `$a2`/`$a3`, `$ra`, `$s2..$s6`): TT `func_8014C5F8` (`or a3,a0,zero` copy), `func_8014CFEC`, `func_8014D168` (loop bound `li s2,128` hoisted before the switch constants; the 128 twice with `u32` counter and a signed selector cast, but in the wrong register order), ETC `func_80142A4C`, KANGEI `func_80136970`, DATE `func_80136F2C`, `func_80137D1C` (base address in `$v1`).
- The T-0018 rows (selector or variable register `$v0`/`$v1`/`$a0`, operand order): see [[data/t0018-cases]].
## Wave 5 list 4 (T-8040)
Ticket [[tickets/T-8040-wave-5-list-4]]. 24 functions matched (5.7 KB) plus 17 dupes copies in the owned files; the list was the hard leftovers, 18 rows went to [[data/t0018-cases]].
- **Local pointer variables for hoisted global loads.** A function whose original loads five global pointers at the top (TT `func_8014A000`) matches with one local per pointer assigned in that order; the register order is the order of the first assignment (v0, v1, a0, a1, a2), not the declaration order.
- **Counter reuse keeps a 4-trip loop rolled and an unrolled tail simple.** TT `func_8014A000`: `for (i = 0; i < 4; i++)` over a 0x64-byte record stays a loop when the same `i` drives a second 7-trip loop (`aA8 += 0x14`), which unrolls with the pointer left incremented.
- **`(s32)` on a `u8` record index.** `unk_1BC[(s32) D_800E6280.unk_F5F]` makes the 0x38 multiplier a signed constant that is shared with an equal `li a0,56` call argument (SHUGAKU `func_80135DB8`). Without the cast the multiplier goes to `a1`.
- **Narrow constant type decides sharing.** `i != 2U` instead of `i != 2` stops the loop bound from sharing the register of a halfword-stored `2` (TT `func_80149D64`, not matched for another reason).
- **Local struct for a block copy.** Use `s32 w[N]` members (word aligned); a `u8 b[N]` struct gives `lwl/lwr`. Copy through the same base symbol as the siblings (`*(Ev44 *)&D_80120650[0x88] = *(Ev44 *)D_80120650`, SHUGAKU `func_8013425C`).
- **Type of a callee's result.** `GetWorkBase`/`func_800490F0` declared as returning a pointer or `s32` fixed the `v0`/`v1` choice of the constants that follow (TACO `func_80153E78`); statement order inside a halfword-store group (`y1` before `x2`, `y2` before `x3`) fixed the delay-slot scheduling.
- **Oversized out buffer.** TACO `func_80153AA0`: `u8 buf[0xA0]` reproduces the frame (FAKE).
- **Local function-pointer tables** (T-3330) again: ENDING `func_8013B760`, SHOUGATU `func_801407C0` (re-read the selector into a `u32 j` after the call), ENDING `func_8013BC58` (locals `i` before `sel` put the s16 spill at +0x28).
- **Dispatch switches with a jump table** (`J`) match as plain C once the callees have prototypes in the overlay header (EVENT `func_8010A330`, DATE `func_80153280`, MASTER `func_80138088`); verify with the unit sha1, `funcdiff` shows jump-table relocation lines only.
- **Blocked, by cause** (rows in the T-0018 file): post-increment compare (`xori/sltiu/beqz`), switch selector in `$v1`, constants in `$a0/$a1`/`$t8` instead of `$t6`, `func_X(D = const)` chain through `$a0`, `$t9` skipped in record `|=` sequences, shared `lui $at` counters (TAIIKU `func_801368B8`), bit-field flag tests (`sll; bltz`, EVENT `func_8010A6E4`, SHOUGATU `func_80140AD8`), u8 parameter kept in its home slot after `arg3 = 8` (TT `func_80148B6C`, `func_80148C04`).
- **Not C-compilable with IDO:** TACO `func_8015D270` (RotMatrixY-like, `addu rd,rs,$zero` moves) is from a gcc-built SDK object inside the overlay; `configure.py` has no per-object toolchain for overlays. Tooling gap for the report.
## Wave 5, list 3 (T-8030)
Ticket [[tickets/T-8030-wave-5-list-3]]. 30 of the 146 listed functions matched (7512 bytes of 48476); main `parameter_change`, `func_800732F8`, `func_800733D8`, `func_800734C4`, `func_800735B8`, `func_800736AC`; BUNKA_SD `func_80133540`; EVENT `func_80106150`; ETC `func_80143240`, `func_801446E4`, `func_8013E364`, `func_8013F320`; GYOZI `func_8014210C`, `func_8013AA48`; OLH `func_80133EA4`, `func_80133F90`, `func_80134184`, `func_80134254`, `func_80134324`; OPTION `func_8013A334`; TACO `func_8014B2A4`, `func_8014BC80`, `func_8014D3B4`, `func_8014D918`, `func_8014DA6C`, `func_8014E048`, `func_8014EC4C`, `func_8014F044`; TT `func_80148210`, `func_80148714`.

New patterns (matched):
- **TACO actor records at `D_8015EDB4`** (`TcActor`, stride 0x88): the byte offsets of m2c (`unk882`, `unk883`, `unk90A`, `unk98E`, `unkA16`, `unkA9E`, `unkB26`) are fields of records 16 to 20: `unk882` is `[16].unk2`, `unk883` `[16].unk3`, `unk90A` `[17].unk2`, `unk98E` `[17].unk84[2]`, `unkA16` `[18].unk84[2]`, `unkA9E` `[19].unk84[2]`; `unk3` of record n is `0x880 + n * 0x88 + 3`. Written this way the whole animation-state family (`func_8014D918`, `func_8014E048`, `func_8014D3B4`, `func_8014EC4C`, `func_8014DA6C`) matches from the m2c shape (two-range `if (v < a || v >= b) ... else if (v >= a && v < b)` ladders stay literal, the redundant second test is the original's).
- **Four-copy status switches** (OLH `func_80133F90` family, main `func_800732F8` family): `s32 f(void) { switch (D_800E6280.unk_110D) { case 0: ...; D_800E6280.unk_110D += 1; break; case 1: ...; break; } }` with no `return` (implicit int, `or v0,v1,zero` copy). Strings are plain literals (the leading ideographic space of the text is in the literal). A callee that the original calls with a string while main defines `void f(void)` (`func_80066104("...")`) needs `#define MAIN_API_OVERRIDE_func_80066104` and a local `void func_80066104();` (sync_protos `--write` then guards the declaration in `main_api.h`).
- **Direct reads beat a local copy** (TACO `func_8014B2A4`): `if (D[17].unk84[2] == 1) { f(); } if (D[17].unk84[2] == 2) { g(); }` matches, while `v = D[17].unk84[2]; if (v == 1) { f(); v = D[17].unk84[2]; }` flips the operands of one `bne`; `1 == x` does not change an operand order (SHUGAKU `func_80138DD8` stays open).
- **Prologue order by source line** (T-7020 rule, new cases): `p = X; do {` on one line (ETC `func_80143240`) and `i = 0; n = arg0; p = &T[arg1];` on one line (TACO `func_8014BC80`) order the `lui/addiu` pair of the pointer like the original; the permuter found the first one by joining the lines. Both carry a `FAKE` comment (line layout only).
- **Pointer loops written by hand** (ETC `func_80143240`): the original is a manually unrolled loop with two separate induction variables (`i += 4; p += 4`), not a counted loop: `i = 1; p = D_800E7D11; do { if (p[0x14] == 0 && B[i] == 0) return 0; ... i += 4; p += 4; } while (i != 0xD);` with the first slot tested before the loop. The counter must be assigned before the pointer for `$v0`/`$v1`.
- **`xori` boolean compare needs a permuter noise** (TACO `func_8014F044`): `if ((t ^ 0) == 1)` is the only plain-looking form found that swaps the operands of the first `bne` (FAKE); `1 == t`, `(s32)t`, `(u32)t`, `!(t - 1)` do not.
- **Unused local arrays reproduce frame size** (TACO `func_8014EC4C`): `s32 pad[3];` above the rand() spill gives the 12 extra frame bytes (FAKE). A single unused `s32` does the same for 4 or 8 bytes (GYOZI `func_80142A8C` needed `s32 unused;` for the `0x28` slot, not matched for other reasons).
- **Post-increment boolean compare** in an implicit-int function: `s32 func_8013AA48(void) { ...; if (D_800F6474++ == 1) {...} }` gives `xori; sltiu; beqz` (GYOZI).
- `D_800F555B` is `D_800F53A0.girl[i].unk_10[3]` (stride 0x38); `D_800E7D24` is `D_800E7D11[0x13]`.

Unsolved shapes (rows in [[data/t0018-cases]]):
- **Constant shared with the call argument** (GEKO `func_8013CC20`, GYOZI `func_8013AC38`, SHOUGATU `func_8013A2F8`, GYOZI `func_80144A74`): `D = 7; f(7)` where the original stores the byte from `$a0` (`li a0,7; lui at; jal; sb a0,..(at)`) and a multiplier `li a0,0x38` doubles as the argument of the next call. Local variables, chain assignments and `f(D = 7)` all give a separate `li t6`.
- **Selector/value in `$v1`/`$a1` instead of `$v0`** (OPTION `func_80139F8C`, `func_80139878`, BUNKAKEN `func_801428D0`, SHUGAKU `func_80139054`, DATE `func_80145DF0`): `u32` declarations, casts and local copies do not move the register.
- **Shared `lui $at` across symbols**: RPG_BAT `func_8014EBD0`, `func_8014EBA8` (28 and 40 bytes) were tried with scalars and with an `s32[5]` view; both still emit one `lui $at` per store (see [[data/shared-at-groups]]); the whole RPG_BAT group (`func_8014EB58`, `func_8014F230`, `func_8014F500`, ...) is blocked by this, 58 listed functions were skipped up front.
- **TACO ring buffers** (`D_8015E8B0`, `D_8015E9F0`, 16 records of 0x14, index in record 0 `+6`): `func_8013D2A0`/`func_8013D1E0` (register swap `$v0`/`$v1` and an extra `move`), `func_8013D348` (`or v0,zero,zero` before the loop, kept live to the return).
- The two DATE rotation helpers `func_8015A640`, `func_8015A7E0` look like compiled libgte (`RotMatrixX/Y`-style, `multu` with `nop` padding, `D_800DE500` table) and were not tried.
## Wave 5, list 2 (T-8020): 21 functions, 6,192 bytes, 36 files

21 of the 133 listed functions match (about 1 in 6 tried; the list is mostly shapes earlier waves already failed). Matched: main `func_800623E4`, `func_80062840`; DATE `func_8014D818`, `func_8014DE10`, `func_8014ECF0`, `func_8014D000`; DATE2 `func_8013775C`; SHUGAKU `func_8013A314`; ENDING `func_80133030`; MASTER `func_80139810`; GEKO `func_80141514`; RPG_BAT `func_80135D90`, `func_80159D50`; ETC `func_801408F0`; NAME_ENT `func_80143498`; OPTION `func_801371FC`, `func_80133AD8`, `func_80136A50`, `func_80135C0C`; BUNKA_SD `func_80134B88`; OLH `func_801332F8`. New or confirmed patterns:
- **A non-void function that falls off its end explains the `move v0,v1` in a switch's first delay slot** (OLH `func_801332F8`, RPG_BAT `func_80159D50`): the original selector sits in `$v1` with a copy in `$v0`. Declare the function `s32` (change `void` in the overlay header, the C has no `return value`), and the copy appears. The T-0018 rows "switch selector `$v1` with `move v0,v1`" are this case.
- **A selector that must stay in `$v0` and compare unsigned** (BUNKA_SD `func_80134B88`, 17 cases on the GameState word): `switch ((u32)D_800E6280.unk_1104.w)`. `.u` puts the selector in `$v1` with a copy, `.w` alone compares signed (`slti`).
- **A promoted global is written as plain global statements, not a local** (main `func_800623E4`): `D_801230D0 = 0` and `D_801230D0 = 1` in the switch arms, then `if (D_801230D0 != 0)`. A local `v` copied from the global and stored back gives `lui a0; lw v1,0(a0)` and a different register.
- **Two stores of one constant through one register are an array chain** (DATE `func_8014D818`, GEKO `func_80141514`): `D_80120650[4] = D_80120650[0x48] = 8;` where the original has `li v0,8; sb v0,..; ...; sb v0,..` around other statements. `D_80120654` and `D_80120698` are elements 4 and 0x48 of the 0x44-byte table.
- **A constant shared between an index multiply and a call argument** (DATE2 `func_8013775C`): `func_80084D3C(0x38U)` takes the unsigned `0x38` register of the `u8 * 0x38` record index (marked FAKE).
- **GameState arrays give one `lui at` per store** (ETC `func_801408F0`, 50 stores of 0 and -1): `D_800E6280.unk_1228[i] = 0;` with the index worked out from the address matches where separate symbols would be needed. (`unk_1228[0]` is `D_800E74A8`.)
- **Load hoisting across byte read-modify-writes is stopped by a single array symbol** (OPTION `func_801371FC`, `func_80135C0C`, family of 5): the `s16` pair `D_80120912/D_80120914` and the four flag bytes `D_8012093F + k * 0x44` are one object. `extern s16 D_80120912[]` with `D_80120912[0]`, `D_80120912[1]` and `((u8 *)D_80120912)[0x2D + i * 0x44] &= ~0x80` (loop body on the `for` line, FAKE comment as in T-7020) matches; separate symbols are hoisted (`lh` before the flag stores). `func_80133AD8` and `func_80136A50` use a rolled 3-trip loop `for (i = 0; i < 3; i++) D_8011ECD0[0x1C6F + i * 0x44] &= ~0x80;`. The `s32` signed `* 9 / 8` pair stores 912 first and 914 second with no temp variable (a temp shifts the first branch to `$a0/$a1`).
- **Function-pointer tables of 16 and 44 entries** (ENDING `func_80133030`, DATE `func_8014ECF0`) follow the T-3330 idiom unchanged. MASTER `func_80139810` (13-word data table, switch on a `u8`, selector `$v1`) needs two unused locals declared before the table to reach the original frame 0x68 (FAKE).
- **m2c invents call arguments** when a callee has no prototype: NAME_ENT `func_80143498`, RPG_BAT `func_80159D50` and TT `func_801436BC` call `func_8004E93C`, `func_8014B738` (3 arguments), `func_8013F220`, `func_80143580` with the registers the asm really sets; read `$a0..$a3` before each `jal`.
- **Strings and jump tables compile in place now**: `func_8013A314` (string in an else-less if), `func_80143498` (3 strings), `func_8014D000` (5-case jump table, `funcdiff` shows the table relocations only; ninja's sha1 decides).
- **`sync_protos.py --fix` re-inserts three hand-written prototypes** (`func_80045288`, `func_80061A3C`, `func_80062634`) a second time; I added `func_8004F984`, `func_8004E93C`, `D_801208FB`, ... with a small script in the address-sorted place and used `--fix` only to move declarations out of overlay headers. Declaring a main-range symbol in an overlay header and running `--fix` can also add an `MAIN_API_OVERRIDE_` define to another overlay header (EVENT owns data at `D_8012071C`), so check `git diff include` after it.

Left as `INCLUDE_ASM`, new gaps (rows in [[data/t0018-cases]]): the near misses with one reordered instruction (BUNKA_SD `func_801345BC`: the loops and offsets match with `D_8011ED58[i * 0x110 + k]`, the `sb` to `D_8012069A` moves; RPG_BAT `func_80145180`, `func_801453B4`: a `li` in the first delay slot, an `addiu`/`lui` order); `x * 0x44` where the original has `sll/addu/sll` (OPTION `func_80134804`, `func_80134C00`, `func_80134E64`, `func_801368FC`: every spelling gives `li 68; multu`); GameState scalar bases (`lui v0; lbu a0,3935(v0)` against `lui a0; lbu a0,0(a0)`, SHUGAKU `func_80139E8C`, DATE `func_8014DCCC`, BUNKA_SD `func_801389DC`); hoisted constants in other registers (TT `func_801436BC`: `$a1/$a2/$a3` against `$t1/$t0/$a3`; EVENT `func_800FAA04`, `func_800F9730`, TACO `func_80147444`, MASTER `func_8013B20C`: an unused `$t5`); a promoted global in `$v1` that IDO keeps in `$v0` (DATE `func_8014E38C`, main `func_800626B0` with or without an `s32` return or a `(u32)` cast); `D_800F62CF = 2; func_8008F618(2)` sharing `$a0` (GYOZI `func_8013B43C`, `func_8013C118`).
## Wave 5, list 8 (T-8080)
Ticket [[tickets/T-8080-wave-5-list-8]]. Patterns that turned T-0018 and "known gap" rows into matches; check `funcdiff.py --resolve` (plain `funcdiff.py` hides `lb`/`lbu` differences behind the relocation filter) and then `ninja`.
- **Scene-switch functions return `s32` without a value.** A `switch` on an unsigned global whose asm has `lbu v1; ...; beqz v1; or v0,v1,zero` (the copy of the switch temporary in the delay slot) is an `s32`/`int` function that falls off the end: the temporary stays live in $v0 as the return value, the cvt pass keeps the copy. Write `s32 f(void) { switch (D) {...} }` with `return;` in the cases and change the header from `void`. RPG_BAT's eleven scene scripts (`func_80136D50`, `func_801375F8`, `func_80137E74`, `func_801480F0`, `func_80158240`, ...) are the same function with other constants; `func_8005BAE0`, `func_80132884` (OLH) too. Without the copy (`void`) the selector goes to $v0.
- **`*(u8 *)&D_800E6280.field` is the scalar-global access.** The original reads these bytes as plain `u8` globals (`LOD`), so the cvt pass moves the selector to $v1, widens counters (`lbu v0; addiu; andi t8; sb t8; or v0,t8,zero`) and keeps the old value in the delay slot. A struct member (`D_800E6280.unk_110D`) is an `ILOD` and gets none of it. Use the u8 view on the switch selector (`func_8005A0B0`, `func_8005D804`, `func_8005C968`, ETC `func_80149BC0`, `func_801495CC`) and on counters that are incremented in an expression (`func_80059E00`, `func_8005A1A0`: `*(u8 *)&D_800E6280.unk_110D += 1`). A field the original reads with `lbu` but the struct declares `s8` needs the same view or a `u8` local copy (`func_8005ECB8`: `u8 sel; sel = field; switch (sel)` keeps it in $v0 like the original, FAKE).
- **Local function-pointer tables with an index passed along.** `tbl.f[idx](0x40, idx)` with `u16 idx` (T-3330 idiom plus the second argument) puts `idx` in $a1 as the original does (SHOUGATU `func_80134930`, GYOZI `func_80137798`); tables whose call has no argument need no extra argument (EVENT `func_800FEA38` with an unused `s32 idx` above the table). Tables that are only read (BUNKAKEN `func_80152F4C`) still need `s32 pad[2]` before them.
- **Stack slots and frame sizes.** Spilled locals and local arrays take the top of the frame in declaration order (first declared = highest address). To move an array down without changing the frame, declare an unused `s32 pad` first; four `s16[4]` arrays (x, y, w, h of `menu_set`) are `s16 x[4]` even for two or three entries (8-byte spacing) with `s32 pad[2]` above them (OPTION-style menus `func_8005E7F0`, `func_8005EA38`, `func_8005EF44`, `func_8005F26C` with `s32 pad; s32 i;`). The loop `for (i = 0; i < 2 or 3; i++) { x[i] = -4; y[i] = -0x40 + i * 0x10; w[i] = 0x6C; h[i] = 0x10; }` matches the original pointer loop (three trips) or its full unrolling (four trips).
- **Uninitialised locals that the original reads.** NAME_ENT `func_80137608` reads two `s32` stack slots that nothing writes: `s32 pad; s32 sp30; s32 sp2C; s32 pad2;` reproduces the layout. `func_80138338`/`func_80138A7C` keep `D_8011ECF6`/`D_8011ECFA` in two spilled locals across a call (`s32 pad; s32 x; s32 y;`).
- **`(u8)` cast on `func_8002328C(..) & 0x7F`** reserves the same extra temp as for `func_8005E0E0` (EVENT `func_801146B4`, `func_80117A68`).
- **Constants shared between a compare and a store in EVENT.** `func_80119440`: `s32 one; if ((one = 1) == D_8012531C) { ... D_8012439C = one; ... }` (permuter result, FAKE) shares the `li` register; the plain forms differ only in the operand order of one `bne`.
- **Word read of a byte global.** EVENT `func_8011C6C4` switches on `*(u32 *)&D_80125320` (the asm uses `lw`; the symbol is `u8` elsewhere).
- **Wrong declared width.** ENDING `D_8013C35C` is read only with `lh`/`sh` (44 times) but declared `s32` in `include/ovl/ENDING.h`: `s16` is right (not changed here, the functions that need it stay open).
- **Not matched, new gaps** (rows in [[data/t0018-cases]]): a `nop` kept after a load where IDO fills the slot with the next `lui` (`join_club_select`, GYOZI `func_801381DC`); a counter tested for zero and then reloaded (ENDING `func_80137F64` family); `idx * 6` recomputed with `multu` for every access (EVENT `func_801142E8` family); store order of two pairs in `func_8005BCEC`.

## Shared `lui $at`: data defined in the file (T-9010)
Ticket [[tickets/T-9010-per-object-data-shared-lui-at]]. Correction of (b) in "Shared constants, shared `lui $at` and loop unrolling" (T-5020) and of the T-7000 note: **IDO 5.3 does share `$at`**, by itself, between stores to different offsets of one symbol when that symbol is defined in the same file. Measured with `cc -O2 -Wo,-nokpicopt -cckr` on scratch files: `short arr[4] = {...}` (initialised global), `static short sarr[4]`, a struct `S st = {...}`, and an uninitialised `static int y[4]` (`.lcomm`) all give one `lui $at` for `arr[0..3] = ...`; an `extern`, a tentative definition `int x[4];` (common in K&R mode) and two different variables (`a1`, `a2`, or two statics `s1`, `s3` of one section) re-emit `lui $at` per store. The T-5020 measurement had used externs and scalar statics. This is exactly the original's rule (T-7000: shared groups are data only the sharing file uses; `GameState` fields, reached from every unit, are never shared; no conflict inside one object), so no as1 pass is needed: the C file that owns the data has to define it ([[decompile-workflow]], "Data defined in the C file").

Other measured facts that the data tools rely on:
- IDO emits a file's `.data` in source order and starts every data definition on a multiple of 4 (`char a[1]` at 0, `char b[5]` at 4, `char c[3]` at 0xC); the section is aligned and padded to 16. An unaligned splat symbol in the original data is therefore a member of the variable before it.
- A member of a defined struct compiles like a scalar for a switch selector (`switch (D_8015EDB0.unk_00)`: `lw v0`), an array element does not (`switch (D_8015EDB0[0])`: `lw v1`, 5 matched RPG_BAT functions changed). Use a struct when other code switches on the members.
- Defining the data does not change the T-5010 selector rule: an entry switch on a defined `u8` still gets `$v1` from `tools/cvt_pass.py` (without the pass, IDO gives `$v0` for both). A scan of the original's entry selectors (`lbu`/`lhu` into `$v0`/`$v1` before the first branch or call, then a compare) against the T-9010 data ranges: data in the function's own object 22 `$v0` to 2 `$v1`; data of another object 132 to 265; objects without a range 126 to 31. So the `$v0` cases that cvt_pass gets wrong are mostly unit-private data, as the U0 note in `tools/queue.py` says. A cvt_pass rule that leaves variables defined in the file alone is a candidate (not made here; cvt_pass is not in this ticket).
- TACO `func_80143E80` (`D_8015F5F0[0..3]`, value 0x400 at index 1): IDO schedules the store of the `li` value first in all 24 source orders; the original stores index 0 first. Stays asm.

Matched (7): RPG_BAT `func_8014EBA8`, `func_8014EBD0`, `func_8014EB58`, `func_8014F230`, `func_8014F500`, `func_8014F524`; main `func_80046290`.

## T-0018 rows under K&R: re-test, clusters, pass verdict (T-9000)
Ticket [[tickets/T-9000-t0018-register-order-rule-or-build-step]]. Question: after the move to K&R (`-cckr`, T-7000), which rows of [[data/t0018-cases]] are now plain C, which shapes are left, and does a uniform ucode pass (like `tools/cvt_pass.py`) reproduce any of them?

### Method
- Row state: 683 rows, 537 distinct functions; 237 are C now (matched by later waves), 300 are still `INCLUDE_ASM` or `NON_MATCHING`.
- Drafts: the C bodies the wave agents left in their scratch directories (602 distinct bodies for 291 of the open functions). A scratch harness (not committed) splices each body over the function's `INCLUDE_ASM` line, applies `migrate_globals.rewrite_text` (old `D_800E6xxx` names), adds the agent's own `extern` lines when cfe reports an undefined symbol, compiles with the build's `cc.compile_ido` (K&R, cvt pass, frame pass) and runs `funcdiff.py --resolve --built`. Header return types can be overridden per test (`#define f f_hdr` before the includes).
- Result: 201 of the 291 functions compile (the rest are raw m2c drafts); 2 match unchanged (RPG_BAT `func_80144640`, DATE `func_80145DF0`). The diffs were then classified by shape (same opcodes with other registers, same multiset in another order, extra or missing opcodes).

### Rows that are source idioms (21 matched, 20 in the build)
- **Implicit `int` (K&R function without a return value).** Retyping every `void` draft to `s32` (no `return`) matched 10 more functions: SHUGAKU `func_80134024`, `func_8013A980`, GEKO `func_8013E56C`, `func_80140EBC`, SHOUGATU `func_80138158`, DATE `func_80148924`, RPG_BAT `func_80142544`, TACO `func_8014E1A0`, OLH `func_80136210` (plus its string literal). Two asm signatures point to it:
  - `xori v0,v1,k; sltiu v0,v0,1; ...; beqz v0` for `if (D++ == k)`: IDO branches on the `xori` result (`bnez`) in a `void` function and keeps the materialised compare when `$v0` is live at the end (an `int` function that falls off its end). The 8 matched functions with this shape in the original are all `s32` without a return (GEKO `func_8013A5E8`, `func_80135A6C`, GYOZI `func_8013AA48`, ...). This is the T-3002 "`D++ == K`" shape; it is solved.
  - The switch-temporary copy `or v0,v1,zero` in a delay slot (T-1321, T-8080), also for OLH `func_80136210`.
  The m2c drafts compare the new value (`D += 1; if (D == k)`); the original compares the old one (`if (D++ == k)`).
- **Flag set as a bit-field store** (T-6010 idiom): `((GirlFlag4 *)&girl[i].unk_10[0])->f = 1` instead of `|= 4` removes the "skipped temporary" (`lbu t0` where IDO gives `t9`) in all nine GYOZI/SHOUGATU rows of that family. `GirlFlag4` is in `include/ovl/GYOZI.h`; SHOUGATU's `Bits64B8` moved from two `.c` files to `include/ovl/SHOUGATU.h`.
- **Chain assignment to array elements** (T-7000 idiom): EVENT `func_8010AC40`, `D_800EAFA0[4] = D_800EAFA0[0x48] = 8;`.
- `func_80148924` writes the `Rec24` fields (`D_801217D0[4].unk_06`, ...) instead of byte offsets from `D_80121866`.
- Not in the build: RPG_BAT `func_80142544` matches as `s32` but reads `D_8012121C` as `s16`, and `include/ovl/EVENT.h` declares that main-exe symbol `s32`; it needs the 8a declaration settled first.

### Clusters of the open rows
By shape of the best draft (181 open functions with a compiling draft):
| shape | functions | what decides it | uniform pass? |
|---|---|---|---|
| extra or missing opcodes, other | 53 | mixed: wrong draft, frames, loops | - |
| `andi 0xff` into the value register (`ori t7; andi v0,t7,0xff; sb v0`) | 27 | the original keeps the narrow global (or a `u8` copy) in a register and truncates after each change | no, see below |
| one `li` per store where the original keeps the constant (`+li`) | 24 | constant kept in a register across straight-line stores | no, see below |
| registers and order both differ | 17 | follows from a register difference | - |
| `nop` only | 13 | mostly a consequence of another register or order difference | no |
| `move` only (`or v0,..`; the `return 0` in a `jr` slot) | 10 | implicit int (fixed above) or the slot choice at a return | no |
| `$v0`/`$v1`/`$aN` against a temporary, `$aN` rotation, `$v0`/`$v1` swap, temporaries shifted | 8 + 8 + 5 + 5 | uopt colouring order | no |
| `li k; multu` against shifts | 5 | the original keeps the stride constant in a register (T-0017) | no |
| order only | 4 | as1 schedule | no |
By symptom text (all 397 open rows): `a0`-`a3` order 72, phantom registers and kept constants 62, other 60, selector copy and `$v0`/`$v1` selector 54, global kept across blocks 51, temporary order 33, `nop`/delay slot 31, `$v0`/`$v1` of a value 20, frame 10, operand order 4.

### Per cluster: the property and the pass verdict
- **Selector copy `or v0,v1,zero`.** Source: an `int` function without a return value (above). Where the selector is loaded after a call or branch and the original still has `$v1` (U1), nothing in IDO's input changes the allocation (T-5010). No pass.
- **Unsigned narrow global kept across blocks** (including the `andi` cluster). Property: uopt's register-promotion priority (the benefit of a live range against its save cost), not visible in the ucode. Evidence that no ucode-local rule separates it: of the narrow unsigned globals that a function loads before its first branch and also stores, the original keeps 81 in a register and leaves 80 in temporaries among the matched functions alone (IDO already reproduces both); the sharpest case is one variable: main `bustup_wink` (matched) and ENDING `func_80137574` are both `if (!(D_8011F4CA & 1)) D_8011F4CA |= k` (k = 0x11 and 3); the original gives `ori t8,v0,0x11; sb t8` in the first and `ori t7,v0,3; andi v0,t7,0xff; sb v0` in the second. The ucode of the two differs only in the constant, so whatever made the second keep a truncated `u8` in `$v0` (possibly a `u8` local in the source; IDO compiles `u8 v = D; if (!(v & 1)) { v |= 3; D = v; }` without the `andi`, tested) is not something a ucode rule can see. The same `andi` shape is in main `func_80067DD4`, `func_80067DFC` and GEKO `func_80134538`. Same verdict as T-1321/T-5010.
- **`a0`-`a3` order and "phantom" registers.** Property: uopt's colouring order, which depends on every other live range in the procedure. Example: main `func_8005E150` has the same loop as the matched `func_8005E7F0`; only its later `get_k_speed()` switch differs, and IDO then gives the loop pointers one register later (`a0..a3, v1` against the original's `v1, a0..a2, v0`); `-Wo,-zcopy:0`, an `s32` selector or no local change nothing. A pass would have to redo the colouring.
- **Constants kept in a register.** Two sources:
  - Chain assignment. cfe writes `a = b = k` on scalars as `LDC k; STR b; LOD b; STR a` (no `LOC` record between), and uopt's constant propagation turns the `LOD b` into a new `LDC`, so ugen loads the constant once per store. For indirect stores (array elements, or `*&x`) uopt keeps one register. EVENT `func_8011B5A8` stores 0x80 to `D_800EC1A6, A5, A4, CA, C9, C8` and `D_800E9E63` from one `$v0`, in descending address order inside each RGB triple: that is `r = g = b = 0x80` evaluated right to left. Written as a chain of `*&D_x` targets, IDO keeps `$v0` but schedules the first store into the `jal` delay slot (the original puts the last one there); a rewrite to the indirect form in a pre-uopt pass would carry that same order difference, and DATE `func_8013DCC0` keeps its three chain constants in ugen temporaries (`t6..t8`), not in uopt registers. Not adopted; the evidence is recorded for a later attempt.
  - Straight-line constants (`li a0,56; multu` twice where IDO shifts and adds; one `li` reused by separate statements): uopt's constant-in-register policy, unchanged by every option (T-0017, T-3001). The same variables are stored with one `li` per statement in matched code (DATE `func_80154D38`), so it is not a property of the data.
- **`nop` kept in a delay slot, the `return 0` slot.** The original fills a `jr` slot with the last store and moves the value move up in 8 sites (main `func_80042808` family, `normal_date_girl_in_init`) and keeps the value move in the slot in 16 (matched `k_disp_inc` and others); IDO matches both kinds elsewhere. No as1 rule; the remaining `nop` rows follow from a different register choice before the slot.
- **Temporary order** beyond the bit-field case (ETC `func_80140AE4`, DATE `func_80149F48`, TT `func_80133AD0`): ugen numbers temporaries round robin, so one extra expression in the original's ucode shifts every later temporary by one. Which expression is extra is per function (the bit-field case was one); no general rule found.

### What a pass would need
IDO 5.2, which carries the original's version stamp 3.18, generates the same code as 5.3 (T-3110), so the remaining differences come from a differently built or configured uopt (likely Sony's toolchain build of the 3.18 suite), and they live in register allocation and constant propagation, not in a ucode record that a pass can add or delete. A faithful build step would have to be a re-implementation of uopt's colouring with the original's priorities, checked against every matched function, which T-1321's threshold patches already failed. Until the original uopt (or its options) is found, these rows stay asm. The idioms above go to [[decompile-workflow]].
## Wave-5 codegen shapes (T-9020)
Ticket [[tickets/T-9020-wave-5-codegen-shapes]]. Eight shapes that wave 5 left open, each checked against IDO 5.3 with the build's own pipeline. Five have a plain C idiom, none needed a build step; 13 functions matched (4227 -> 4240). How-to summary: [[decompile-workflow]], "Wave-5 codegen shapes".
1. **`x * 0x44` as `sll 4; addu; sll 2`** (OPTION `func_80134804` family). It is not a source multiply (every spelling of `sel * 0x44` or `&tbl[sel]` gives `li 68; multu`): uopt's linear-function test replacement makes it when a loop over 0x44-byte records compares its counter with a selector, `if (i == sel)`, and the record pointer becomes the only induction variable. That happens when the stored value runs on its own counter (`for (i = 0, j = 0; i < 3; i++, j += 2) ... = j + 0x1B`); with `i * 2 + 0x1B` uopt keeps `2i` as the induction variable and compares `2i` with `2 * sel`. The records are a file-local `OptSpr44` view (`s16` at +0x18) of the `D_8011ECD0` table. A store to `D_801208FB` followed by loads of `D_8011ECF6`/`D_8011ECFA` keeps its order only when all three go through `D_8011ECD0` (as1 hoists a load over a store to another symbol, not over one through the same symbol, [[game-state]] "Views"): more evidence that `D_8011ECD0` is one object. Matched: `func_80134804`, `func_80134C00`, `func_80134E64`, `func_801368FC`.
2. **A constant loaded once and stored several times** (T-3001 (a)). IDO keeps a chain assignment's value in one register when the targets are array or record elements (T-7000), and that is the shape: `D_800CA224[0] = D_800CA224[1] = D_800CA224[2] = 3;`, `x[0] = x[1] = -0x80;` for local menu arrays. Stores come out innermost target last (scheduled to the end), the others right to left. A scalar target anywhere in the chain re-loads the constant per store. The `D_800CA21C`..`D_800CA238` scalars are four `s16[4]` rows (`D_800CA21C`, `D_800CA224`, `D_800CA22C`, `D_800CA234`): DATE indexes each row from its own base (`&D_800CA22C[i]`), so one 2-D array is wrong (the original folds the row base into the relocation). Matched: DATE `func_8013DCC0`, `func_80137FAC`, TAIIKU `func_80141964`, main `normal_date_two_select_init` (the last one needs an unused `s32 pad`, FAKE). Left (rows in [[data/t0018-cases]]): `normal_date_three_select_init`, `normal_date_girl_suddenin`, DATE `func_8013FA6C`, `func_8013BEB8`, all with the chain right and one register or one `li`/`lui` order off.
3. **ENDING counter reloaded after its zero test** (`func_80137F64`). `if (D_8013C360 == 0) { } else if (D_8013C360 == 1)` reloads the counter as the original does; `if (D != 0) { if (D == 1)` keeps one register. The counter is unsigned (`u32`): its compares load their own `li at,1` while the `s16` selector's `1` stays in `$v1`. `D_8013C35C` is `s16` (read only with `lh`). The siblings `func_80138C70`, `func_80138A9C`, `func_80138DC8` need only the `u32` declaration. `D_8011F4D0/F4DE/F4E0/F522` are fields +0x18/+0x26/+0x28 of records 30 and 31 of the `D_8011ECD0` table; read through the table (`*(s16 *)&D_8011ECD0[0x80E]`) they compare in the original's operand order. Left: `func_80137E40` (register pair swapped).
4. **A constant shared between a store and a call argument** (`li a0,7; lui at; jal f; sb a0,D`). `f(*(u8 *)&D_800E6280.unk_F5F = 7)` (or any element/indirect target) gives the shared `$a0`; a struct member, a scalar or a local does not. What is left is the order of `li a0` and `lui at`: the original puts `li` first at all 37 such sites (all unmatched), IDO's as1 puts the `lui` first for this chain. Same family: DATE `func_8013FA6C`, SHOUGATU `func_80140AD8`. No C form found; an as1 scheduling question, not a source one. Nothing matched.
5. **Bit-field flag tests on the Rec38 flag word**: one type now. `CharFlags` (`include/main_api.h`) names bits 0-8, the 3-bit value at 9-11 and bits 12-14; `Rec38.unk_0C` is the union `Rec38Flags` (`w`, `u`, `h`, `b`, `f`). Evidence: the original tests bits 1 (68 sites), 2 (54), 3, 6, 14 with `sll; bltz/bgez` and reads 9-11 with `sll 20; srl 29`. The thirteen file-local views (`KangeiFlagBits`, `Flags14`, `Bits64B8`, ...) are replaced by `D_800E6280.unk_1BC[i].unk_0C.f.bN`; every user builds the same bytes (the byte-wide `u8` view of `Bits64B8` included). `x.f.b14 >= 1` is the original's `sltiu; xori` (ENDING `func_80137E40`).
6. **TACO struct-by-value frames**: no hidden struct type. `func_801563E4` passes three `TcPos` locals and spills an `s32` at sp+0x58/0x50/0x48/0x40 (frame 0x60), the same slots that the T-8060 `FAKE` pads reproduced. The family declares `TcPos p0, p1, p2; s32 r;` (or `p1, p2, r` in the 0x58 frames) and uses some of them; the four matched functions now declare those locals instead of `FAKE` pads (separate locals, not an array: the first argument's local is the highest slot).
7. **TACO `func_8015D270`** (and DATE `func_8015A640`, `func_8015A7E0`, three rotation helpers of the `RotMatrixX/Y/Z` kind on the `D_800DE500` sine table) is PsyQ-style gcc + aspsx output (`addu rd,rs,$zero` moves, `j` for a local jump, `nop` after `mflo`). Per-object compiler choice is a few lines in `configure.py` (a path-to-toolchain map for overlay C files, like `C_TOOLCHAIN` for main), but the native arm64 image has no old gcc (`/opt/gcc` is empty; decompals/old-gcc ships x86_64 builds only), so the build could not compile such an object on Apple Silicon. Not done; needs old-gcc built for arm64 first (follow-up).
8. **GameState members "read as separate scalars"** (list 1): the T-7010 verdict holds, none is a view problem. main `func_80042960` matches with the T-8080 rule (`s32` function without a value, `switch (*(u8 *)&D_800E6280.unk_1108)`). VALEN `func_80133670` (with a `{lo:4; hi:5}` view of `unk_0F8`) differs only in the base register of one `lbu`; BUNKASAI `func_80146030` needs the selector both hoisted (plain read) and in `$v1` (`*(u32 *)&` view), which no form gives together; TACO `func_8013546C` misses on an `s8` local's sign extension. Rows in [[data/t0018-cases]].

## Wave 6, list 3 (T-9130)
Ticket [[tickets/T-9130-wave-6-list-3]]. 37 functions matched (10,736 bytes); 27 rows added to [[data/t0018-cases]]. New or confirmed patterns:
- **GameState member names carry three digits below 0x100.** `D_800E62BA` is `D_800E6280.unk_03A`, `D_800E62BE` is `unk_03E`; `unk_3A` is a member of another struct and compiles with a warning to the wrong offset. Look the field up in `main_api.h` before writing a cast (`*(u8 *)&D_800E6280.unk_3E` produced a wrong "load order" difference in DATE2 `func_80137A2C`; the plain `unk_03E` matched at once).
- **A constant-second compare needs an array, not a scalar.** Three flags at `D_8015EBCC/EBD0/EBD4` tested with `== 1` give `bne value,const` for scalars but the original has `bne const,value`: declare `extern s32 D_8015EBCC[3]` and test `[0]`, `[1]`, `[2]` (RPG_BAT `func_801513C8`). Same ILOD rule as the TAIIKU `D_801491E0[4]` note in "Selector register rule (T-5010)".
- **`s32` state function without a return** also covers GameState bytes: `if (unk_03A >= 3U) unk_03A -= 2; else {...}` in an `s32 f(void)` gives the `or v0,t6,zero` copy (OPTION `func_801387E4`); the companion `func_80132AB8` is plain `if (unk_03A >= 2U) unk_03A -= 2;` with three tests of the member. RPG_BAT switch dispatchers (`func_8014F790`, `func_801504E0`) and TACO `func_8013587C` follow the same rule.
- **A rolled loop of 256 stores 0x20 apart is a `0x20`-step loop** (TT `func_80142D40`, the note in the loops section): `for (i = 0; i < 0x2000; i += 0x20) *(s16 *)(p + i) = 0;`. The `RECT` needs the w/h one-line `FAKE` of the neighbour.
- **Local string and table frames need a pad above them** when the original frame has 4 or 12 bytes above the local: `s32 pad;` before `u8 buf[3] = "..."` (SHUGAKU `func_8013799C`, DATE2 `func_801378A4`), before a 0xCC3-byte `DateTxt` copy (DATE2 `func_80137A2C`), `u8 b` after `u8 a` for byte locals at sp+0x2B/0x29 (GYOZI `func_80140AFC`), `s32 pad[3]` between two spilled pointers (TT `func_8013CA0C`). All `FAKE` comments; the real source declared other locals.
- **Local fptab with two tables** (KANGEI `func_80138158`): `s32 idx; FnTbl67 a; FnTbl68 b;` matches; the scalar above both tables is the 4 bytes of the T-3330 rule. A table with a `u8 v` after it (EVENT `func_800FD00C`) fixes a frame 8 bytes short but the `lui v1` of the final load stays different (permuter 10 with a dead `if (1) {}`).
- **Repeated `D_800B0A04[k].unk_xx` offsets**: EVENT scenes touch `D_800B0A04[2]`, `[4]`, `[6]`, `[9]` (`D_800B0A6E` = `[2].unk_02`, `D_800B0B3E` = `[6].unk_02`, `D_800B0BDA` = `[9].unk_02`). A scalar `D_800B094E` that sits before the table needs the `*(s16 *)((u8 *)D_800B0A04 - 0xB6)` form of the neighbour (EVENT `func_801071D8`).
- **Compare chains read from an aggregate view**: `RpgRec18 D_8015EC58` supplies `D_8015EC5C..6C` (`func_80151984`); a 41-pointer string table copied to a local with three drawn indices as `s32 a, b, c; StrTbl41 tbl;` (declaration order a, b, c, tbl) puts them at sp+0xD4, 0xD0, 0xCC and the table at sp+0x28 (RPG_BAT `func_80150F84`).
- **Not buildable: gcc-compiled SDK code inside overlays.** DATE `func_8015A980` (`RotMatrixZ`-like, `addu rd,rs,$zero`, `negu`, `j`, `nop` after `mflo`) and TT `func_8014D260` are PsyQ gcc output like the TACO `func_8015D270` family; only IDO is wired for overlays.
- **Known gaps hit again** (rows in [[data/t0018-cases]]): selector `$v1` vs `$v0` (BUNKAKEN `func_80140F60`, RPG_BAT `func_80150B90`), swap of two globals in `$v1/$v0` (main `func_80086640`), `$a0` shared constants in init sequences (GYOZI `func_8013AC38`, `func_8013D294`), address-CSE of `&D_800E6280` (main `icon_disp_switch`), phantom `$a1/$a2` constants (TT `func_801436BC`), global promoted into `$v0` for the return value (SHUGAKU `func_801385A8`), `* 0x38` via `multu` (SHOUGATU `func_8013BC2C`).
